110	   *  仅初速 >8 时激活（正牌出生速=瞄准向×5 恒不触发——973 甩剑才用） */
111	  terra?: { ai0: number; ai1: number; ai2: number };
112	  /** 星怒剑 503（aiStyle 5 :22139-22157）：targetY=目标线（鼠标 Y 与玩家
113	   *  cy−200 取小）；线上方穿墙/alpha 渐显钳 150，线下开始撞块 */
114	  star?: { targetY: number };
115	}
116	
117	/** extraUpdates：已并入 vanilla-projectiles.json（tools/extract-projectiles.mjs
118	 *  NUM_FIELDS 提取，249 款非 0；83 眼激光=2 等原先手工条目同源于 SetDefaults） */
119	
120	/** 旋转模式（scripts/_projrot-audit.mjs 对 AI_001 type 链逐分支提取 + 非 aiStyle1
121	 *  特例）：默认 'up' = 贴图朝上（AI_001 尾部默认 atan2+π/2，:54877——箭/子弹）；
122	 *  下表 = 贴图【朝右】的弹型（rotation=atan2(vy,vx)，向左运动时按原版
123	 *  spriteDirection 水平镜像，食人鱼 AI 即 :26122-26140 模式）：
124	 *  16 魔法飞弹头（113 Magic Missile，AI_009 :54039 ToRotation；54×54 方图头朝右）、
125	 *  34 Flamelash 火鞭（218，AI_020 族 ToRotation；48×384 八帧行按帧行切片）、
126	 *  190 食人鱼（aiStyle 39，1156 食人鱼枪；曾恒 +π/2 → 鱼 90° 侧翻）、
127	 *  837（AI_001 显式 MIRROR 分支 :54715，1313 骷髅头法书 shoot）、
128	 *  1023（AI_001 仅 wiggle :54743，基姿态 0 朝右，5460 发射器） */
129	const PROJ_ROT_RIGHT = new Set([16, 34, 190, 837, 1023]);
130	export { PROJ_ROT_RIGHT };
131	
132	export class Arrow extends Entity {
133	  w = 10; h = 10; // 原版 SetDefaults type 1：width/height = 10；构造器按弹型覆写
134	  vx: number;
135	  vy: number;
136	  damage: number;
137	  knockback: number;
138	  /** 原版投射物类型（1=木箭 2=燃烧箭，PickAmmo projToShoot = ammo.shoot） */
139	  projId: number;
140	  /** 绘制 scale（SetDefaults scale 字段；绘制尺寸 = 贴图原生 × scale，
141	   *  与判定盒 w/h 无关——子弹 14 是 2×20 曳光条 × 1.2，曾误画成 10×100） */
142	  drawScale = 1;
143	  /** 回收掉落的 item key（null = 不回收，如燃烧箭） */
144	  dropKey: string | null;
145	  grav: number;
146	  life: number;
147	  pierce: number;
148	  /** 发射时 maxPenetrate（穿透判定用——剩 1 的穿透弹仍是穿透语义,Projectile.cs:11904） */
149	  pierceInit: number;
150	  /** 敌对弹（原版 Projectile.hostile）：命中玩家结算（Damage_EVP 语义） */
151	  hostile: boolean;
152	  /** 随从/哨兵射出的弹（ProjectileID.Sets.MinionShot/SentryShot 语义：吃鞭 tag） */
153	  whipTagShot = false;
154	  /** 命中施加 OnFire 300t（1106 火舌 :11002-11004） */
155	  ignite = false;
156	  /** 暴击加成（百分点，spawn 侧注入：player.critChance(kind)+item.crit；基 4% 另计。
157	   *  审计 §6：此前硬编码 4% 导致远程/魔法/投掷吃不到装备/套装/词缀/item.crit */
158	  critBonus = 0;
159	  /** 暴击总概率阈值（0-1，spawn 侧一次性算好；未设=按 critBonus+4%） */
160	  critChance = 0;
161	  /** 护甲穿透（N:81913 减防语义——spawn 注入，命中时传 hurt 第 5 参） */
162	  armorPen = 0;
163	  /** 星云套 booster 回调（StrikeNPC :12892-12905：魔法弹命中 → Game 判定掉落） */
164	  nebulaCb: (() => void) | null = null;
165	  /** aiStyle 14 弹跳：撞块反弹不消亡 */
166	  bounce: boolean;
167	  /** 冰霜盔甲引擎资格（ranged 弹 true;魔法/召唤弹 false——原版 melee||ranged 门） */
168	  frostEligible = false;
169	  /** aiStyle 14 荆棘球档（277）：撞块按 Projectile.cs:18306-18314 反弹 */
170	  thornBounce: boolean;
171	  /** 延迟重力（275/276）：age 超过此值才施加 grav；-1=不延迟 */
172	  gravDelay: number;
173	  /** 专家追踪参数（null=无） */
174	  homing: { speed: number; weight: number; floor?: number; cap?: number } | null;
175	  /** 原版 Projectile.extraUpdates：每帧额外子步数（0=普通 1 步） */
176	  extraUpdates: number;
177	  /** X 轴空气阻力/tick（1=无；投掷族 0.97） */
178	  drag: number;
179	  /** 终端下落速度（缺省 16） */
180	  maxFall: number;
181	  /** 翻滚旋转（aiStyle 2 刀族重力期） */
182	  tumble: boolean;
183	  /** 平飞期姿态锁定（48/54/93/520/599 前 20t atan2 姿态，Projectile.cs:21971-21972） */
184	  tumblePoseLock: boolean;
185	  /** 翻滚累积角（tumble 专用，勿与 Enemy.spin 混） */
186	  tumbleRot = 0;
187	  /** 已存活 tick（延迟重力/追踪门用） */
188	  age = 0;
189	  // ---- 泰拉刃光束 985（aiStyle 191）专属态 ----
190	  terra = false;
191	  // ---- 星怒剑 503 专属态 ----
192	  /** 目标线 Y（null=非星怒弹）；cy>targetY 后 tileCollide 生效（:22139-22143） */
193	  starY: number | null = null;
194	  /** alpha（255 起 −15/t；线上钳 150 / 线下钳 0——:22197-22206） */
195	  starAlpha = 255;
196	  /** 周期音（Item9 :22177-22180，20-60t 掷一次） */
197	  private starSndT = 20;
198	  /** tileCollide 已生效（越目标线后） */
199	  private starCollide = false;
200	  /** localAI[0] 计时 */
201	  terraT = 0;
202	  /** ai[0] 朝向（±1）/ ai[1] 生命基准 18 / ai[2] 物品 scale（出生注入） */
203	  terraAi0 = 1;
204	  terraAi1 = 18;
205	  terraAi2 = 1;
206	  /** 尾段清伤（t ≥ Lerp(ai1, ai1+25, 0.65) → damage=0，:39337-39340） */
207	  terraNoHit = false;
208	  /** localAI[1] 近墙减速标记 */
209	  terraWall = false;
210	  /** 已反射（原版 Projectile.reflected：反射源命中后置位，防重复反射；
211	   *  反射后弹体转 hostile——不再入下方敌怪判定，改走 hitPlayer 伤玩家） */
212	  reflected = false;
213	  /** 穿透投射物的同敌免疫表（敌人 id 集合） */
214	  private hitSet = new Set<number>();
215	  // ---- 喵刀弹 502（Meowmere Cat）专属态 ----
216	  /** ai[0]：弹跳+命中共享计数（0..5，≥5 消亡）——弹跳 :18169 递增/命中 :16794
217	   *  递增同槽（先到 5 者杀弹，喵叫音高 style=5+ai0 随之爬升） */
218	  meowCount = 0;
219	  /** localNPCHitCooldown 同敌再命中冷却（SetDefaults :5466-5468 = 10t） */
220	  private meowHitCd = new Map<number, number>();
221	  /** oldPos 拖尾环（彩虹拖尾 Main.cs:32495-32513 沿 oldPos 逐段绘制，N=10） */
222	  private meowTrail: number[] = [];
223	  /** 迪斯科 RGB（DoUpdate_AnimateDiscoRGB Main.cs:19441 六段 ±7/帧；初值 R=255） */
224	  private discoR = 255;
225	  private discoG = 0;
226	  private discoB = 0;
227	  private discoStyle = 0;
228	  /** 弹体点光（Game 实体光扫读 lightRGB——同 Enemy 约定） */
229	  lightRGB: [number, number, number] | null = null;
230	  /** 点光源像素覆盖（缺省=实体中心；985 泰拉刃 :39382 光心=弹心+朝向 85×scale） */
231	  lightRGBAt: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;
232	  dead = false;
233	
234	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number,
235	    knockback: number, projId = 1, dropKey: string | null = null, opts?: ArrowOpts) {
236	    super();
237	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
238	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
239	    this.damage = damage;
240	    this.knockback = knockback;
241	    this.projId = projId;
242	    this.dropKey = dropKey;
243	    // 判定盒/绘制 scale 按弹型取 SetDefaults（子弹 14 = 4×4 hitbox；箭 1 = 10×10）
244	    const pd0 = projectileData(projId);
245	    if (pd0?.width) this.w = pd0.width;
246	    if (pd0?.height) this.h = pd0.height;
247	    this.drawScale = pd0?.scale ?? 1;
248	    this.grav = opts?.grav ?? 0.3;
249	    this.life = opts?.life ?? 1200;
250	    this.pierce = opts?.pierce ?? 1;
251	    this.pierceInit = this.pierce;
252	    this.hostile = opts?.hostile ?? false;
253	    this.bounce = opts?.bounce ?? false;
254	    this.thornBounce = opts?.thornBounce ?? false;
255	    this.gravDelay = opts?.gravDelay ?? -1;
256	    this.homing = opts?.homing ?? null;
257	    this.extraUpdates = opts?.extraUpdates ?? pd0?.extraUpdates ?? 0;   // 子弹 14=1(2 步/tick)、高速弹 207=2
258	    this.drag = opts?.drag ?? 1;
259	    this.maxFall = opts?.maxFall ?? 16;
260	    this.tumble = opts?.tumble ?? false;
261	    this.tumblePoseLock = opts?.tumblePoseLock ?? false;
262	    this.terra = !!opts?.terra;
263	    if (opts?.terra) {
264	      this.terraAi0 = opts.terra.ai0;
265	      this.terraAi1 = opts.terra.ai1;
266	      this.terraAi2 = opts.terra.ai2;
267	    }
268	    if (opts?.star) this.starY = opts.star.targetY;
269	  }
270	
271	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
272	    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标——
273	    // 曾双变换把箭甩出屏幕,表现为"箭隐形但能命中")
274	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
275	    if (!ctx) return;
276	    // 泰拉刃光束 985 独占（DrawProj_TerraBlade2Shot Main.cs:27670-27712）：
277	    // Frame(1,4) 四帧 170×170（帧 0 主体/帧 3 白芯）；多层镜像叠画：
278	    // 蓝(45,124,205)×2 对称 ±π/8 张开 + 绿白内芯 ×2 + 绿(34,177,76) ×2
279	    // ±0.25 收拢 + 黄绿(181,230,29) + 帧 3 白芯三层递缩——ai[0]=0（未传参）
280	    // 时各 ±ai0 项归零对称；旋转角=velocity 角（无 +π/2，贴图正交）。
281	    // 淡出 = Remap(localAI[0],0,25,1,0)
282	    if (this.terra) {
283	      const img = projSprite(985);
284	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
285	      const fw = img.naturalWidth, fh = img.naturalHeight / 4;
286	      // 淡入淡出（:39344 985 专属）：Remap(t,0,ai1/2,0,1)×Remap(t,ai1+13,ai1+25,1,0)
287	      const aIn = Math.min(1, this.terraT / (this.terraAi1 * 0.5));
288	      const aOut = this.terraT <= this.terraAi1 + 13 ? 1
289	        : Math.max(0, (this.terraAi1 + 25 - this.terraT) / 12);
290	      const fade = aIn * aOut;
291	      // num6 双段（:39273 主段 Remap(t,ai1*0.4,ai1+25,0,1)——绘制张合的推进分母）
292	      const num = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (this.terraT - this.terraAi1 * 0.4) / (this.terraAi1 + 25 - this.terraAi1 * 0.4)));
293	      const spread = 1 - num;
294	      // 绘制 scale（:39341）：Remap(1−(1−num6)²,0,1,1.5,1)×ai2
295	      const fv = 1 - (1 - num) * (1 - num);
296	      const terraScale = (1.5 - 0.5 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, fv))) * this.terraAi2;
297	      const rot = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
298	      const layer = (sy: number, a: number, angOff: number, frame: number, flipV: boolean) => {
299	        ctx.save();
300	        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, a * fade));
301	        ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
302	        ctx.rotate(rot + angOff * this.terraAi0);   // ±ai[0] 方向项（:27694 ai0=±1）
303	        if (flipV) ctx.scale(1, -1);
304	        const dw = fw * sy * terraScale;
305	        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, frame * fh, fw, fh, -dw / 2, -fh * sy * terraScale / 2, dw, fh * sy * terraScale);
306	        ctx.restore();
307	        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
308	      };
309	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
310	      layer(0.95, 0.5, Math.PI / 8 * spread, 0, false);            // 蓝镜像对（±π/8×spread）
311	      layer(0.95, 0.5, -Math.PI / 8 * spread, 0, true);
312	      layer(1, 0.12, 0, 0, false);                                 // 绿白内芯（color5 近似）
313	      layer(1, 0.3, Math.PI / 4 * 0.25 * spread, 0, false);        // 绿 ±0.25 收拢
314	      layer(1, 0.3, -Math.PI / 4 * 0.25 * spread, 0, true);
315	      layer(0.975, 0.5, Math.PI / 4 * 0.15 * spread, 0, false);    // 黄绿主芯
316	      layer(1, 0.6, Math.PI / 4 * 0.05 * spread, 3, false);        // 帧 3 白芯三层
317	      layer(0.8, 0.5, -0.05, 3, false);
318	      layer(0.6, 0.4, -0.1, 3, false);
319	      return;
320	    }
321	    // 星怒剑 503 独占：rotation=vel−π/2（:22236-22239），origin (w/2, 70)
322	    // （Main.cs:29898-29901——34×90 贴图锚点近底部，剑尖前伸 70px）；
323	    // Opacity = 1−alpha/255 渐显
324	    if (this.starY !== null) {
325	      const img = projSprite(503);
326	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
327	      ctx.save();
328	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - this.starAlpha / 255));
329	      ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
330	      ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) - Math.PI / 2);
331	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
332	      ctx.drawImage(img, -img.naturalWidth / 2, -70, img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight);
333	      ctx.restore();
334	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
335	      return;
336	    }
337	    // 翻滚态用累积角（aiStyle 2）；48/54/93/520/599 平飞期姿态锁定 atan2
338	    const tumbling = this.tumble && !(this.tumblePoseLock && this.age <= this.gravDelay);
339	    const ang = tumbling ? this.tumbleRot : Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
340	    const img = projSprite(this.projId);
341	    // 喵刀弹 502 彩虹拖尾（Main.cs:32495-32513）：Projectile_250.png（14×32）沿
342	    // oldPos 逐段——旋转=段向-π/2、纵向拉伸=段长/32、alpha=127/255×(1-i/N)，
343	    // 画在【本体之前】（原版在本体绘制后叠画，此处先画拖尾再本体等效）
344	    if (this.projId === 502 && this.meowTrail.length >= 4) {
345	      const streak = projSprite(250);
346	      if (streak && streak.complete && streak.naturalWidth > 0) {
347	        const N = this.meowTrail.length / 2;
348	        for (let i = N - 1; i > 0; i--) {
349	          const x1 = this.meowTrail[i * 2], y1 = this.meowTrail[i * 2 + 1];
350	          const x2 = this.meowTrail[(i - 1) * 2], y2 = this.meowTrail[(i - 1) * 2 + 1];
351	          const segLen = Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1);
352	          if (segLen < 0.01) continue;
353	          const segAng = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1) - Math.PI / 2;
354	          ctx.save();
355	          ctx.translate(x1 + this.w / 2, y1 + this.h / 2);
356	          ctx.rotate(segAng);
357	          ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
358	          ctx.globalAlpha = (127 / 255) * (1 - i / N);
359	          // origin (w/2, 0)：段起点为锚、沿段向拉伸到段长（scaleY=段长/贴图高）
360	          ctx.drawImage(streak, -streak.naturalWidth / 2, 0,
361	            streak.naturalWidth, Math.max(1, segLen));
362	          ctx.restore();
363	          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
364	        }
365	      }
366	    }
367	    ctx.save();
368	    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
369	    // 朝右贴图族（PROJ_ROT_RIGHT）：rotation=atan2(vy,vx)（vanilla :26122-26140 模式），
370	    // 向左运动水平镜像（spriteDirection 语义）；其余默认朝上 atan2+π/2（AI_001 L54877）
371	    const rightArt = PROJ_ROT_RIGHT.has(this.projId);
372	    if (rightArt && this.vx < 0) {
373	      ctx.scale(-1, 1);              // 先镜像再旋转（R(π−ang)∘M ≡ 原版 flip+atan2(−vy,−vx)）
374	      ctx.rotate(Math.PI - ang);
375	    } else {
376	      ctx.rotate(rightArt ? ang : ang + Math.PI / 2);
377	    }
378	    // 502 朝向（:22659-22665）：spriteDirection=sign(vx)——贴图在旋转坐标系内水平镜像
379	    if (this.projId === 502 && this.vx < 0) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
380	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
381	    // 多帧行（190 食人鱼 4 帧/837 3 帧）按帧行切片防胶片压扁；单帧走整图
382	    const frames = projFrameCount(this.projId);
383	    const frameImg = frames > 1 ? projFrameImg(this.projId, Math.floor(this.age / 5) % frames) : null;
384	    const drawImg = frameImg ?? img;
385	    if (drawImg && (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).complete !== false
386	      && (drawImg.width > 0 || (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalWidth > 0)) {
387	      // 原生尺寸 × SetDefaults scale（与判定盒 w/h 解耦）：子弹 14 = 2×20×1.2
388	      // 曳光条；箭 1 = 14×32 贴图 × 1。曾统一拉成 w×w 宽 → 100px 巨型子弹/箭偏小
389	      const s = this.drawScale;
390	      const iw = drawImg.width || (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalWidth;
391	      const ih = drawImg.height || (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalHeight;
392	      ctx.drawImage(drawImg as CanvasImageSource, -iw * s / 2, -ih * s / 2, iw * s, ih * s);
393	    } else {
394	      // 贴图未就绪：短线兜底
395	      ctx.strokeStyle = this.projId === 2 ? '#FFB060' : '#D8C8A0';
396	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
397	      ctx.beginPath();
398	      ctx.moveTo(0, 0);
399	      ctx.lineTo(0, -Math.min(14, Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) * 1.4));
400	      ctx.stroke();
401	    }
402	    ctx.restore();
403	  }
404	
405	  /** 统一消亡出口（Projectile.Kill 语义钩子）：碎镜 1085 近身 150px 坏运等
406	   *  按弹型分发由 Game 侧 onProjectileDeath 消费 */
407	  private killNow(game: GameHooks): void {
408	    this.dead = true;
409	    game.onProjectileDeath?.(this.projId, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
410	  }
411	
412	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
413	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：对敌判定归拥有者客户端；对玩家（hostile）由访客本地结算 Damage_EVP（netPuppetProjContact）
414	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
415	    // extraUpdates 子步循环（Projectile.cs:15331-15336）：numUpdates = extraUpdates，
416	    // while(numUpdates>=0) 跑完整 AI/位移/命中体——timeLeft 也在循环内逐子步消耗
417	    //（:15861），故弹体寿命同步缩短为 1/(N+1)。
418	    for (let u = 0; u <= this.extraUpdates; u++) {
419	      this.subStep(game);
420	      if (this.dead) return;
421	    }
422	  }
423	
424	  /** 单次子步（无 extraUpdates 时即整帧本体） */
425	  private subStep(game: GameHooks): void {
426	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
427	    const world = game.world;
428	    this.age++;
429	    // aiStyle 1/2 通用重力（原版箭 0.3/tick 抛物线；直飞弹 grav=0）；
430	    // 275/276 延迟重力（Projectile.cs:54318-54329：35t 后 g=0.025）；
431	    // aiStyle 2 投掷族（Projectile.cs:21955-21977 默认档）：20t 平飞后
432	    // vy+=0.4、vx×=0.97，终端 32
433	    if (this.grav !== 0 && (this.gravDelay < 0 || this.age > this.gravDelay)) {
434	      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + this.grav, this.maxFall);
435	      if (this.drag !== 1) this.vx *= this.drag;
436	    }
437	    // 翻滚（aiStyle 2 刀族 :21508，自出生每 tick 累加）；48/54/93/520/599 在
438	    // 平飞期由 draw 侧姿态锁定覆盖（:21971-21972）
439	    if (this.tumble) {
440	      this.tumbleRot += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
441	    }
442	    // 泰拉刃光束 985（AI_191 :39248-39266 + 985 专属尾段 :39333-39377）：
443	    // 寿命 = ai[1]+25（出生 18 → 43t）；淡入 ai[1]/2=9t、末 12t 淡出（:39344）；
444	    // t≥Lerp(ai1,ai1+25,0.65)≈34 清伤（纯视觉尾段）；>8 速才减速/探墙（正牌
445	    // 出生速=瞄准向×5 恒不触发，973 甩剑共用段）
446	    if (this.terra) {
447	      this.terraT++;
448	      const terraLife = this.terraAi1 + 25;
449	      if (this.terraT >= terraLife) { this.killNow(game); return; }
450	      if (!this.terraNoHit && this.terraT >= Math.round(this.terraAi1 + 25 * 0.65)) {
451	        this.terraNoHit = true;
452	        this.damage = 0;                                  // :39337-39340 damage=0
453	      }
454	      const stT = world.store;
455	      this.lightRGB = [0.25, 0.86, 0.38];    // Color(64,220,96)（:39382）
456	      // 光心 = 弹心 + 朝向 ×85×scale（:39382 Center+rotation.ToRotationVector2()*85*scale）
457	      {
458	        const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) || 1;
459	        this.lightRGBAt = { x: this.cx + (this.vx / sp) * 85, y: this.cy + (this.vy / sp) * 85 };
460	      }
461	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 0.5 && Math.random() < 0.6) {
462	        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#7CE81E', 1, 1.2, { life: 18 });
463	      }
464	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 8) {
465	        this.vx *= 0.94; this.vy *= 0.94;
466	        if (!this.terraWall) {
467	          const rot = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
468	          let clear = false;
469	          for (let k = -1; k <= 1 && !clear; k += 0.5) {
470	            const a = rot + k * (Math.PI / 4) * 0.25;
471	            clear = canHit(stT, this.cx, this.cy, 0, 0,
472	              this.cx + Math.cos(a) * 110, this.cy + Math.sin(a) * 110, 0, 0);
473	          }
474	          if (!clear) this.terraWall = true;
475	        }
476	        if (this.terraWall && Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 8) { this.vx *= 0.8; this.vy *= 0.8; }
477	        if (this.terraWall) { this.vx *= 0.88; this.vy *= 0.88; }
478	      }
479	    }
480	    // 星怒剑 503（aiStyle 5 :22139-22157 + 503 段 :22197-22266）：无重力恒速；
481	    // 线上方穿墙（tileCollide=false）→ 越线后撞块；alpha 255−15/t 渐显（线上
482	    // 钳 150 / 线下钳 0）；周期 Item9 星啸；尘 58 星尘尾；rotation=vel−π/2（draw 侧）
483	    if (this.starY !== null) {
484	      // :22139-22143 tileCollide=true 仅当 Center.Y > ai[1]（严格大于——边界对齐）
485	      this.starCollide = this.cy > this.starY;
486	      const above = this.cy < this.starY;
487	      const floorA = above ? 150 : 0;
488	      this.starAlpha = Math.max(floorA, this.starAlpha - 15);
489	      if (--this.starSndT <= 0) {
490	        this.starSndT = 20 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40);
491	        game.playSfxFiles(['Item_9'], 0.5, this.cx, this.cy);
492	      }
493	      if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
494	        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FFF8C8', 1, 0.9, { life: 20 });
495	      }
496	    }
497	    // 专家追踪（Projectile.cs:54330-54345 275/276 / :23307-23316 277：
498	    // v=(v*(weight-1)+dirToPlayer*speed)/weight；<floor 或 >cap 归一）
499	    if (this.homing) {
500	      const p = game.player;
501	      if (p && !p.dead) {
502	        let dx = p.cx - (this.x + this.w / 2), dy = p.cy - (this.y + this.h / 2);
503	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
504	        dx = dx / d * this.homing.speed;
505	        dy = dy / d * this.homing.speed;
506	        this.vx = (this.vx * (this.homing.weight - 1) + dx) / this.homing.weight;
507	        this.vy = (this.vy * (this.homing.weight - 1) + dy) / this.homing.weight;
508	        const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
509	        if (this.homing.floor !== undefined && sp < this.homing.floor && sp > 0) {
510	          this.vx = this.vx / sp * this.homing.floor;
511	          this.vy = this.vy / sp * this.homing.floor;
512	        }
513	        if (this.homing.cap !== undefined && sp > this.homing.cap) {
514	          this.vx = this.vx / sp * this.homing.cap;
515	          this.vy = this.vy / sp * this.homing.cap;
516	        }
517	      }
518	    }
519	    this.x += this.vx;
520	    this.y += this.vy;
521	    // 喵刀弹 502：迪斯科点光（Projectile.cs:22613-22621 (Disco+0.5)/2）+ 拖尾采样
522	    if (this.projId === 502) {
523	      const n = 7;   // DoUpdate_AnimateDiscoRGB（Main.cs:19441-19496 六段 ±7/帧）
524	      switch (this.discoStyle) {
525	        case 0: this.discoG = Math.min(255, this.discoG + n); if (this.discoG >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
526	        case 1: this.discoR = Math.max(0, this.discoR - n); if (this.discoR <= 0) this.discoStyle++; break;
527	        case 2: this.discoB = Math.min(255, this.discoB + n); if (this.discoB >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
528	        case 3: this.discoG = Math.max(0, this.discoG - n); if (this.discoG <= 0) this.discoStyle++; break;
529	        case 4: this.discoR = Math.min(255, this.discoR + n); if (this.discoR >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
530	        default: this.discoB = Math.max(0, this.discoB - n); if (this.discoB <= 0) this.discoStyle = 0; break;
531	      }
532	      this.lightRGB = [(0.5 + this.discoR / 255) / 2, (0.5 + this.discoG / 255) / 2, (0.5 + this.discoB / 255) / 2];
533	      this.meowTrail.push(this.x, this.y);
534	      if (this.meowTrail.length > 20) this.meowTrail.splice(0, this.meowTrail.length - 20);   // oldPos 10 点
535	      for (const [k, v] of this.meowHitCd) {
536	        if (v <= 1) this.meowHitCd.delete(k); else this.meowHitCd.set(k, v - 1);
537	      }
538	    }
539	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
540	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
541	    if (!world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.killNow(game); return; }
542	    const tileType = world.store.get(tx, ty);
543	    // 星怒剑线上方穿墙（:22139-22143 越线前 tileCollide=false）——整段跳过
544	    if (tileType !== 0 && !(this.starY !== null && !this.starCollide)) {
545	      // 可砍物（杂草/瓦罐）：Projectile.CutTiles 语义——弹幕扫过即砍
546	      game.cutTile(tx, ty);
547	      // 阻挡判定只看【实心】(Main.tileSolid 语义):树干/火把/平台等非实心格
548	      // 箭直接穿过(此前 tileType!==0 一刀切,箭会被树挡住——树 solid:false)
549	      if (!world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) return;
550	      // aiStyle 14 弹跳弹（希腊火 326-328 / 装饰球 346）：撞块法向反弹不消亡
551	      if (this.bounce && this.bounceOff(game)) return;
552	      // 喵刀弹 502（AI_008 弹跳档 :18165-18206）：tink 音 + ai[0] 计数 ≥5 消亡；
553	      // 全速翻面（无 ×0.9 衰减——type 15 才有 ×0.8），同 hitSet 语义无
554	      if (this.projId === 502 && this.meowBounceOff(game)) return;
555	      // 荆棘球 277 专属档（Projectile.cs:18306-18314）：vx 恒反 ×0.9；
556	      // 仅入撞 |vy|>3 竖弹 ×0.9（贴地滚动）
557	      if (this.thornBounce && this.thornBounceOff(game)) return;
558	      // 实心块：1/3 概率回收掉落（原版箭 Kill 的掉落），然后消失
559	      if (this.dropKey && Math.random() < 1 / 3) game.spawnDrop(this.x, this.y, this.dropKey, 1);
560	      this.killNow(game);
561	      return;
562	    }
563	    // 敌对弹命中玩家（原版 Projectile.Damage_EVP :13706-13830）：不消耗弹体，
564	    // Player.damage 自带 iframes 去重（= 原版 player.immune 门禁）；
565	    // 实际造成伤害才走 StatusPlayer 的 debuff 授予（:13798-13800；
566	    // 276 毒种子的中毒授予在 statusPlayer case 276）
567	    if (this.hostile && hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage)) statusPlayer(game, this.projId);
568	    // 敌对弹命中城镇 NPC(原版 flag2 分支:hostile && victim.friendly,Projectile.cs:11975-11976);
569	    // 不消耗弹体(原版该分支不动 keepIterating)
570	    if (this.hostile) hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, 0, 'hostile');
571	    // 反射后的弹体 friendly 已翻（原版 Damage NPC 需 friendly 门禁，小动物也是 NPC）
572	    // ——不再伤小动物/敌怪，只走上方 hitPlayer 回打发射者
573	    if (this.reflected) return;
574	    // 小动物:一击致死并消耗弹体(原版小动物是 NPC;近战挥砍同语义)
575	    if (hitCritters(this, game)) { this.killNow(game); return; }
576	    // 命中敌人（原版 penetrate：箭 1 射中即停；手里剑 4 穿 4 敌，同敌免疫防连击）
577	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
578	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; id: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks, pen?: number, crit?: boolean, pierce?: number, fromPlayer?: boolean, penPercent?: number) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] }; reflectsProjectiles?: boolean };
579	      if (e.dead) continue;
580	      if (this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y) {
581	        // 反射源（419 旋刃冲刺等 reflectsProjectiles 置标）：命中前先走反射
582	        // （原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060，反射即终止本帧命中迭代 keepIterating=false）
583	        if (tryReflectProjectile(this, this.projId, e, game)) {
584	          this.pierce = 1;            // 原版 penetrate=1（NPC.cs:67058）
585	          this.pierceInit = 1;        // 反射后按单发弹语义（命中不设敌方帧）
586	          this.hitSet.clear();        // 转敌对后命中表作废
587	          return;
588	        }
589	        // 502 喵刀弹：localNPCHitCooldown=10t 同敌再命中（穿透弹 hitSet 是永久免疫，
590	        // 502 是"同一敌 10t 冷却后可再咬"——SetDefaults :5466-5468）
591	        if (this.projId === 502) {
592	          const cd = this.meowHitCd.get(e.id) ?? 0;
593	          if (cd > 0) continue;
594	          this.meowHitCd.set(e.id, 10);
595	        } else if (this.hitSet.has(e.id)) continue; // 穿透弹已命中过此敌
596	        // ★鞭 tag（WhipTagEffect.CanRunHitEffects :73-84：MinionShot/SentryShot 弹幕吃
597	        //   tag——随从/哨兵射出的箭 whipTagShot 置标时走 resolveWhipTagHit 统一结算：
598	        //   ModifyTaggedHit tag 加伤+TagCrit 暴击（WhipTagEffect.cs:58-70）、OnTaggedHit
599	        //   （黑收成 916）、proc 窗口首跳（TagEffectState.cs:216-243）。随从弹无基础
600	        //   暴击（原版 summon 系 crit 仅来自 TagCrit）——4% 基础暴击只留给玩家远程弹）
601	        let dmg = this.damage;
602	        let crit: boolean;
603	        if (this.whipTagShot) {
604	          const en2 = e as unknown as import('./WhipTag').WhipTagged;
605	          // 黑收成 OnTaggedHit 直伤通道（916 等价：额外一跳落在被命中敌上）
606	          en2.hurtFx = (d: number) => { e.hurt(d, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, -2, game); };
607	          const res = resolveWhipTagHit(en2, dmg, game,
608	            (el) => (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(el, 'projectiles'),
609	            SUMMON_TAG_MUL[this.projId] ?? 1, e.x + e.w / 2, e.y + e.h / 2);
610	          dmg = res.dmg;
611	          crit = res.crit;
612	        } else {
613	          // 暴击：基 4% + spawn 注入的系暴击/item.crit（P:2300-2304/P:25230-25232）
614	          crit = Math.random() < (this.critChance || (4 + this.critBonus) / 100);
615	          if (crit) dmg *= 2;
616	        }
617	        if (this.ignite) {
618	          const en3 = e as unknown as { onFireT?: number };
619	          en3.onFireT = Math.max(en3.onFireT ?? 0, 5); // AddBuff(24,300)=5s
620	        }
621	        // 命中 debuff（StatusNPC:10555 表驱动——火枪弹 2 33% OnFire 180t 等,按型号掷骰）
622	        applyProjStatus(this.projId, e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
623	        // 冰霜盔甲引擎(ranged 门,:93712;魔法/召唤弹 frostEligible=false)
624	        if (this.frostEligible) {
625	          applyFrostBurn((game.player as unknown as { equipStats: { frostBurn: boolean } }).equipStats.frostBurn,
626	            e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
627	        }
628	        if (this.nebulaCb) this.nebulaCb(); // 星云 booster（:12892-12905，伤害>0 门已过）
629	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
630	        e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game, this.armorPen, crit, this.pierceInit, true);
631	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');
632	        this.hitSet.add(e.id);
633	        // 502 喵刀弹命中：无特效音/无计数消亡（全库唯一 PlaySound(37) 在撞块
634	        // HandleMovement :16797——2026-08-13 实测复核修正：曾误接在命中链）。
635	        // 命中只消费 penetrate（5 NPC 上限）+ localNPCHitCooldown 同敌冷却
636	        // ---- 吸血链（Projectile.cs:12877-12892 on-hit 段）----
637	        // 吸血鬼飞刀 304 → vampireHeal（:12879-12882，!moonLeech 门）；
638	        // 魔法弹 + 幽灵套 ghostHeal → ghostHeal（:12883-12888，canGhostHeal 全 true
639	        // 近似——雕像产怪 flag 未实装）。ghostHurt（幽灵伤害头 156）未接，登记。
640	        // numHits = 含本次的命中数（原版 StrikeNPC 先自增，hitSet.size 此时已含本敌）
641	        if (this.projId === 304) {
642	          game.applyLifeSteal?.('vampire', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y);
643	        } else if (projectileData(this.projId)?.magic) {
644	          game.applyLifeSteal?.('ghost', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y, true);
645	        }
646	        if (--this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
647	      }
648	    }
649	    // 城镇 NPC 受击(原版 Projectile.Damage 对 friendly NPC 同样生效——杀裁缝师
650	    // 召骷髅王即此链);穿透/销毁语义与敌怪一致
651	    if (hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2)
652	      && --this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
653	  }
654	
655	  /** 荆棘球 277 撞块反弹（Projectile.cs:18306-18314）：vx 恒反 ×0.9；
656	   *  竖向仅入撞 |vy|>3 才 ×-0.9（否则贴地滚动）。逐轴回退探测，脱困失败 false。 */
657	  private thornBounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
658	    const st = game.world.store;
659	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
660	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
661	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
662	    };
663	    let bounced = false;
664	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
665	      this.x -= this.vx;
666	      this.vx *= -0.9;
667	      bounced = true;
668	    }
669	    if (this.vy > 3 && !solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
670	      this.y -= this.vy;
671	      this.vy *= -0.9;
672	      bounced = true;
673	    }
674	    return bounced;
675	  }
676	
677	  /** aiStyle 14 撞块反弹（Projectile.cs:18314-18327 默认档：法向 ×-0.5，低速归零）。
678	   *  逐轴回退探测穿透轴并反弹；两轴均无法脱困（嵌入过深）则返回 false 交还消亡。 */
679	  private bounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
680	    const st = game.world.store;
681	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
682	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
683	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
684	    };
685	    let bounced = false;
686	    // X 轴：回退一步脱离实心 → 穿透轴是 X，反弹并退回
687	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
688	      this.x -= this.vx;
689	      this.vx *= -0.5;
690	      bounced = true;
691	    }
692	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
693	      this.y -= this.vy;
694	      this.vy = this.vy > 1 ? -this.vy * 0.5 : 0;
695	      bounced = true;
696	    }
697	    return bounced;
698	  }
699	
700	  /** 喵刀弹 502 撞块档（HandleMovement :16794-16812——type 502 专支，先于
701	   *  通用 aiStyle-8 tink 链（:18165）命中，故弹跳只喵叫不 tink）：
702	   *  ai[0]=Clamp(+1,1,5) + PlaySound(37=Meowmere, style 5+ai0)（Item_57/58
703	   *  随机、音量 ×0.5×style×0.05（style 6-10 → 0.15-0.25）、音高扰动 ±0.4
704	   *  由双素材近似）；≥5 消亡；全速翻面（与 lastVelocity 逐轴对比后取负）。 */
705	  private meowBounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
706	    const st = game.world.store;
707	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
708	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
709	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
710	    };
711	    this.meowCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, this.meowCount + 1));   // Clamp(:16796)
712	    const style = 5 + this.meowCount;
713	    game.playSfxFiles([Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'Item_57' : 'Item_58'],
714	      0.5 * style * 0.05, this.cx, this.cy);
715	    if (this.meowCount >= 5) { this.killNow(game); return true; }
716	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
717	      this.x -= this.vx;
718	      this.vx = -this.vx;
719	    }
720	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
721	      this.y -= this.vy;
722	      this.vy = -this.vy;
723	    }

23	/** hslToRgb（原版 Main.hslToRgb 近似——S=1 L=0.5 时的纯色环） */
24	function hueToRgb(h: number): [number, number, number] {
25	  h = ((h % 1) + 1) % 1;
26	  const c = 1, x = c * (1 - Math.abs((h * 6) % 2 - 1));
27	  let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
28	  const seg = Math.floor(h * 6);
29	  if (seg === 0) { r = c; g = x; } else if (seg === 1) { r = x; g = c; }
30	  else if (seg === 2) { g = c; b = x; } else if (seg === 3) { g = x; b = c; }
31	  else if (seg === 4) { r = x; b = c; } else { r = c; b = x; }
32	  return [Math.round(r * 255), Math.round(g * 255), Math.round(b * 255)];
33	}

35	export class PrismProng extends Entity {
36	  w = 18; h = 18;
37	  /** ai[0] 棱序 0-5 */

64	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
65	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
66	    const p = this.parent;

70	    let num723 = 20, num725 = 15, num726 = -2, num724 = 1;
71	    if (charge < 180) {
72	      num724 = 1 - charge / 180;
73	      num725 = 20 - charge / 180 * 14;
74	      if (charge < 120) {
75	        num723 = 20 - 4 * (charge / 120);
76	        this.opacity = charge / 120 * 0.4;
77	      } else {
78	        num723 = 16 - 10 * ((charge - 120) / 60);
79	        this.opacity = 0.4 + (charge - 120) / 60 * 0.6;
80	      }
81	      num726 = -22 + charge / 180 * 20;
82	    } else {
83	      num724 = 0; num723 = 1.75; num725 = 6;
84	      this.opacity = 1; num726 = -2;
85	    }
86	    // ---- 光棱轨道分布（:30368-30376）----
87	    const aim = Math.atan2(p.dirY, p.dirX);
88	    const off = this.idx - 2.5;
89	    const orbit = (charge + off * num723) / (num723 * 6) * Math.PI * 2;
90	    // UnitY.RotatedBy(θ) = (−sinθ, cosθ)（XNA 旋转阵）；num722 取其 .Y=cosθ
91	    const spreadRot = Math.cos(orbit) * (Math.PI / 6) * num724;   // :30376
92	    // 预旋转分量（:30374：(−sinθ,cosθ)×(4,num725)——曾写 sin(θ+π/2) 相位错，修正）
93	    const pxPre = -4 * Math.sin(orbit);
94	    const pyPre = num725 * Math.cos(orbit);
95	    // 轨道偏移旋转到 aim 系（.RotatedBy(aim)）
96	    const ca = Math.cos(aim), sa = Math.sin(aim);
97	    this.x = p.cx - this.w / 2 + ca * 16 + ca * num726 + (ca * pxPre - sa * pyPre);
98	    this.y = p.cy - this.h / 2 + sa * 16 + sa * num726 + (sa * pxPre + ca * pyPre);
99	    // 光束方向 = aim 旋转 spreadRot
100	    const rot = aim + spreadRot;
101	    this.vx = Math.cos(rot);
102	    this.vy = Math.sin(rot);

162	    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
163	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(this.cx, this.cy); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke();
164	    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${a * 0.75})`;
165	    ctx.lineWidth = 13 * this.prongScale * 0.75;
166	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(this.cx, this.cy); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke();
167	    ctx.restore();
168	  }
169	}
170	
171	export class PrismProj extends Entity {
172	  w = 14; h = 18;
173	  /** ai[0] 充能计数 */
174	  charge = 0;
175	  /** 平滑瞄准方向（velocity，模长=shootSpeed 30） */
176	  dirX: number; dirY: number;
177	  damage: number;
178	  knockback: number;
179	  critChance = 0.04;
180	  armorPen = 0;
181	  /** channel 电平 / 瞄准点 / 每拍魔耗（失败=false） */
182	  channelCb: () => boolean;
183	  aimCb: () => { x: number; y: number };
184	  payMana: () => boolean;
185	  prongs: PrismProng[] = [];
186	  dead = false;
187	  private frame = 0;
188	  private spawnGuard = false;
189	
190	  constructor(dirX: number, dirY: number, damage: number, knockback: number,
191	    channelCb: () => boolean, aimCb: () => { x: number; y: number }, payMana: () => boolean) {
192	    super();
193	    const n = Math.hypot(dirX, dirY) || 1;
194	    this.dirX = dirX / n * 30; this.dirY = dirY / n * 30;   // shootSpeed 30
195	    this.damage = damage;
196	    this.knockback = knockback;
197	    this.channelCb = channelCb;
198	    this.aimCb = aimCb;
199	    this.payMana = payMana;
200	  }
201	
202	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
203	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
204	    this.frame++;
205	    // 魔耗节奏（:63628-63636：>90t 15 拍、>120t 5 拍，否则 30 拍）
206	    this.charge++;
207	    const cadence = this.charge > 120 ? 5 : this.charge > 90 ? 15 : 30;
208	    if (this.charge % cadence === 0 && !this.payMana()) { this.dead = true; this.killProngs(); return; }
209	    // aim 平滑（:63662-63676）：value7=Normalize(瞄准向) 先归一，再
210	    // Lerp(value7, Normalize(velocity), 0.92)（曾漏归一直接 0.08×原始差——鼠标
211	    // 距离越远偏差越大，2026-08-13 复查修正）
212	    const aim = this.aimCb();
213	    const pd = Math.hypot(this.dirX, this.dirY) || 1;
214	    let ax = aim.x - this.cx, ay = aim.y - this.cy;
215	    const al = Math.hypot(ax, ay) || 1;
216	    ax /= al; ay /= al;
217	    let mx = ax * 0.08 + (this.dirX / pd) * 0.92;
218	    let my = ay * 0.08 + (this.dirY / pd) * 0.92;
219	    const ml = Math.hypot(mx, my) || 1;
220	    this.dirX = mx / ml * 30; this.dirY = my / ml * 30;
221	    // channel 门（:63700-63717）：断 channel/noItems/CCed → Kill
222	    if (!this.channelCb()) { this.dead = true; this.killProngs(); return; }
223	    // 挂玩家手（heldProj 语义：parent.Center 即光棱轨道圆心）
224	    const pl = game.player as unknown as { cx: number; cy: number } | null;
225	    if (!pl) { this.dead = true; this.killProngs(); return; }
226	    this.x = pl.cx - this.w / 2;
227	    this.y = pl.cy - this.h / 2;
228	    // 首帧生成 6 光棱（:63702-63710）
229	    if (!this.spawnGuard) {
230	      this.spawnGuard = true;
231	      const nd = Math.hypot(this.dirX, this.dirY) || 1;
232	      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
233	        const prong = new PrismProng(i, this.damage, this.knockback);
234	        prong.parent = this;
235	        prong.vx = this.dirX / nd; prong.vy = this.dirY / nd;
236	        prong.critChance = this.critChance;
237	        prong.armorPen = this.armorPen;
238	        this.prongs.push(prong);
239	        (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(prong, 'projectiles');
240	      }
241	    }
242	  }
243	
244	  private killProngs(): void {
245	    for (const q of this.prongs) q.dead = true;
246	  }
247	
248	  draw(r: { canvas: HTMLCanvasElement }): void {
249	    // 晶体本体（Projectile_633.png 26×120 = 5 帧；帧按充能推进≈3t/帧）
250	    const img = projSprite(633);
251	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
252	    if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0 || !ctx) return;
253	    const fh = img.naturalHeight / 5;
254	    const f = Math.floor(this.frame / 3) % 5;
255	    const rot = Math.atan2(this.dirY, this.dirX);
256	    ctx.save();
257	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
258	    ctx.rotate(rot);
259	    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, Math.round(f * fh), img.naturalWidth, Math.round(fh),
260	      -img.naturalWidth / 2, -fh / 2, img.naturalWidth, fh);
261	    ctx.restore();
262	  }
263	}
264	

```

  private useCombatWeapon(cwIn: Exclude<CombatWeapon, { kind: 'melee' }>, def: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number]) {
    const inp = this.input;
    const ang = Math.atan2(inp.mouseY - this.renderer.canvas.height / 2, inp.mouseX - this.renderer.canvas.width / 2);
    const px = this.player.cx + Math.cos(ang) * 14;
    const py = this.player.cy - 4 + Math.sin(ang) * 14;
    // 发射型武器使用期间面向鼠标（ItemCheck_Shoot :46578-46590：num=Dot(朝向轴,瞄准向)
    // >0→1 否则 −1；全 itemAnimation 每 useTime tick 重评。723/3611 豁免（:46563））。
    // ★纯近战阔剑不在此列——挥砍期间方向锁定（:19546-19556 useTurn 门）
    this.player.facing = Math.cos(ang) > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const consume = () => {
      this.player.inv.removeAt(this.player.inv.selected, 1);
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    };
    // 词缀乘区（Item.Prefix :551-557）：damage=round(×dmg)、knockBack×kb——
    // 手雷兜底下限在乘区后取 max，与原版（damage 先乘再判 0）一致；
    // 装备全系伤害乘区（徽章/险恶词缀等）：近战族=melee、魔法=magic、其余=ranged
    const ps = this.heldPrefixStat();
    const dmgKind = cwIn.kind === 'magic' ? 'magic' : (cwIn.kind === 'boomerang' || cwIn.kind === 'spear' || cwIn.kind === 'yoyo' || cwIn.kind === 'flail') ? 'melee' as const : 'ranged' as const;
    const cw = {
      ...cwIn,
      damage: Math.max(1, Math.round(cwIn.damage * (ps?.dmg ?? 1) * this.player.damageMult(dmgKind))), // ⑥ pen 移 hit 时
      knockback: cwIn.knockback * (ps?.kb ?? 1),
      // ⑧ 词缀乘区出口统一折算（I:558-568）：shtspd→shootSpeed、spd→useTime（I:561）
      shootSpeed: ((cwIn as { shootSpeed?: number }).shootSpeed ?? 0) * (ps?.shtspd ?? 1),
      useTime: Math.max(1, Math.round(((cwIn as { useTime?: number }).useTime ?? 0) * (ps?.spd ?? 1))),
    };
    // GetWeaponCrit（Player.cs:42531 族）= 4 基础 + 武器 item.crit + 装备/buff——
    // 投射物近战四族此前写死 4%（Sunfury item.crit=7 原版应 11%）
    const critVid = def.vid ?? viIdFromKey(def.key);
    const critTotal = 0.04 + (itemCombat(critVid)?.crit ?? 0) / 100
    // 成就：蜜蜂套装持蜂枪使用（Player.cs:16060-16069 → event 3 NOT_THE_BEES：
    // head 160 + body 168 + legs 103 且 itemAnimation 中挥动 1121 BeeGun）
    if (critVid === 1121) {
      const b0 = this.player.inv.armor[0], b1 = this.player.inv.armor[1], b2 = this.player.inv.armor[2];
      if (b0 && b1 && b2
        && statOfInternal(b0.id)?.hs === 160 && statOfInternal(b1.id)?.bs === 168
        && statOfInternal(b2.id)?.ls === 103) {
        this.achievements.handleSpecialEvent(3,
          { statLifeMax: this.player.maxHp, statManaMax: this.player.maxMana });
      }
    }
      + this.player.critChance(dmgKind === 'magic' ? 'magic' : dmgKind === 'ranged' ? 'ranged' : 'melee') / 100
      + (ps?.crt ?? 0) / 100; // ⑧ 词缀 crt（I:566 crit+=crt）
    // 使用姿势（Player.ItemCheck：itemAnimation>0 期间持物可见）。
    // ★ spear/yoyo/flail/boomerang 族源码 noUseGraphic=true（DefaultToSpear :47637 等
    //   202 处）——投射物即本体，挥动再画持物会重影，这四族不加 swing
    if (cw.kind === 'grenade' || cw.kind === 'magic' || cw.kind === 'shot' || cw.kind === 'summon') {
      this.useSwing(def, ang);
    }
    switch (cw.kind) {
      case 'grenade': {
        // AI_016 引信 timeLeft 1:1（SetDefaults :10348-10372）：炸弹 28=180 / 炸药棍
        // 29=300 / 手雷 30=180——曾误写 150/135（引信时长与原版不符的根源）
        // 引信 1:1（SetDefaults :10348-10408）：炸药棍族 {29,470,637}=300,余 180
        const fuse = cw.shoot === 470 || cw.shoot === 637 || cw.shoot === 29 ? 300 : 180;
        // 爆炸伤害（PrepareBombToBlow :44134-44145）：炸弹族 {28,37,516}=100 /
        // 炸药棍族 {29,470,637}=250；手雷族 {30,397,517} 用物品伤害（不低于 60）
        const dmg = cw.shoot === 28 || cw.shoot === 37 || cw.shoot === 516 ? 100
          : cw.shoot === 29 || cw.shoot === 470 || cw.shoot === 637 ? 250
            : Math.max(cw.damage, 60);
        // 地形破坏半径（Projectile.cs:75262 表）：炸弹族28/37/516/519=4、炸药棍族
        // 29/470/637=7；**手雷族(30 等)不在 ExplodeTiles 判定表内=不破坏地形**
        const er = Game.EXPLODE_RADIUS[cw.shoot] ?? 0;
        // 实体伤害盒(PrepareBombToBlow Projectile.cs:44125-44220,非 Kill 还原图):
        // 炸弹族 128×128 盒半宽 64 / 炸药棍族 250×250 半宽 125 / 手雷 22×22 半宽 11
        // ——与地形破坏半径【无关】;旧值 11/100 致炸弹 AoE 几乎炸不到实体(2026-08-13 修)
        const hurtBox = cw.shoot === 29 || cw.shoot === 470 || cw.shoot === 637 ? 125
          : cw.shoot === 28 || cw.shoot === 37 || cw.shoot === 516 ? 64 : 11;
        this.entities.add(new GrenadeProj(px, py,
          Math.cos(ang) * cw.shootSpeed, Math.sin(ang) * cw.shootSpeed,
          dmg, fuse, cw.shoot, (ex, ey, edmg) => this.explodeAt(Math.floor(ex / TILE), Math.floor(ey / TILE), edmg, er, er > 0, hurtBox, this.player.equipStats.armorPen + (ps?.arpen ?? 0), cw.shoot)), 'projectiles'); // ⑥+⑧
        consume();
        this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
        this.sfx.play('throw');
        this.input.mouseDown = false; // 消费点击(⑩ 边沿门防连发,与 flail/yoyo/magic 一致)
        break;
      }
      case 'glowstick': {
        // aiStyle 14（AI_014 弹跳物理/粘性粘墙/妖精排斥场）：消耗 1 支掷出；
        // 光照走通用弹幕点光（projectileData.light=1）
        this.entities.add(new GlowstickProj(px, py,
          Math.cos(ang) * cw.shootSpeed, Math.sin(ang) * cw.shootSpeed,
          cw.shoot), 'projectiles');
        consume();
        this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
        this.sfx.play('throw');
        this.input.mouseDown = false;
        break;
      }
      case 'boomerang': {
        // AI_003：飞出减速→返回主角（非消耗，回手即消失）
        const bom = new Boomerang(px, py,
          Math.cos(ang) * cw.shootSpeed, Math.sin(ang) * cw.shootSpeed,
          cw.damage, cw.knockback, cw.shoot);
        bom.critChance = critTotal;
        bom.armorPen = this.player.equipStats.armorPen; // ⑥
        // 301 Bouncing Shield 按 20t/100t 双门需 channel（其余族不用,空挂无害）
        if (cw.shoot === 301) bom.channel = () => this.input.mouseHeld && !this.input.uiBlocking;
        this.entities.add(bom, 'projectiles');
        this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
        this.sfx.play('throw');
        this.input.mouseDown = false;
        break;
      }
      case 'spear': {
        // AI_019：随角色前刺——伸缩包络按型号参数表(AI_019_Spears_GetSpearOffset),位移=offset×shootSpeed
        const spear = new SpearProj(Math.cos(ang), Math.sin(ang), cw.shootSpeed * (ps?.size ?? 1), cw.useTime, // P:46431 num13=shootSpeed×scale(词缀 size)
          cw.damage, cw.knockback, cw.shoot);
        spear.critChance = critTotal;
        spear.armorPen = this.player.equipStats.armorPen + this.player.meleeArmorPen; // ⑥ 近战含磨刀
        this.entities.add(spear, 'projectiles');
        this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
        this.sfx.play('throw');
        this.input.mouseDown = false;
        break;
      }
      case 'whip': {
        // 鞭（ProjectileAI_Whip 主干简化）：从玩家朝鼠标甩出/收回（时长=useAnimation），
        // 命中敌人伤害+tag（UniqueTagEffects TagDamage，240t 内随从对该敌 +N）。
        // ★伤害取武器基伤×词缀（GetWeaponDamage summon → minionDamage 单次乘区）——
        // 不能用 wrapper 预乘后的 cw.damage（那已含 ranged 乘区，WhipProj 再乘
        // summonDamageMult 会双乘；armorPen 同理由命中侧加）
        const whip = cw as Extract<typeof cw, { kind: 'whip' }>;
        const owner = this.player;
        const whipObj = new WhipProj(
          owner.cx, owner.cy, ang, whip.shoot, whip.itemId,
          Math.max(1, Math.round(cwIn.damage * (ps?.dmg ?? 1))),
          whip.knockback, whip.useTime, whip.shootSpeed,
          () => (owner.dead ? null : owner),
          // 月主鞭补鞭实时瞄准（Projectile.cs:45045-45046 朝鼠标方向重掷）
          () => Math.atan2(inp.mouseY - this.renderer.canvas.height / 2,
            inp.mouseX - this.renderer.canvas.width / 2),
        );
        this.entities.add(whipObj, 'projectiles');
        // 鞭攻速（SetItemAnimation :4266：summonerWeaponSpeedBonus × whipUseTimeMultiplier，
        // 封顶 3×——summonerWeaponSpeedBonus 来自鞭 buff 311/308/314，黑曜套给 wutm）
        const swb = (owner.whipBuffs[311] ? 0.35 : 0) + (owner.whipBuffs[308] ? 0.25 : 0)
          + (owner.whipBuffs[314] ? 0.12 : 0);
        const whipAtk = Math.min(3, 1 + swb * owner.equipStats.whipSpd);
        this.player.useTime = Math.max(1, Math.round(whip.useTime / whipAtk));
        whipObj.bonusTagDmg = ps?.tagdmg ?? 0; // I:567 bonusTagDamage
        this.sfx.playWav('whipCrack', 1, owner.cx, owner.cy); // DefaultToWhip UseSound=Item152（鞭响）
        this.input.mouseDown = false;
        break;
      }
      case 'summon': {
        // 召唤：上限链 = Player.maxMinions（1+buff+散件+套装）/ maxSentries；
        // 超限驱逐最旧（原版 FreeUpPetsAndMinions 最便宜优先——本作全 1 槽=最旧先死）；
        // damage 传武器基伤——随从每 tick 按当时装备重算（Projectile.cs:15368 live 刷新）
        const minions = this.entities.projectiles.filter((e) => e instanceof MinionProj && !e.sentry && !(e as MinionProj).noSlot) as MinionProj[];
        // 星尘龙：已有龙头 → 不出新头，尾前插 2 段（SpawnMinionOnCursor :47906-47963）
        if (cw.shoot === 625) {
          const head = minions.find((m) => m.projId === 625);
          if (head && !head.dead) {
            head.extendDragon(this);
            this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
            this.sfx.playWav('summon', 1, this.player.cx, this.player.cy);
            this.input.mouseDown = false;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (!cw.sentry) {
          if (minions.length >= this.player.maxMinions) minions[0].dead = true; // 驱逐最旧
        } else {
          const sentries = this.entities.projectiles.filter((e) => e instanceof MinionProj && e.sentry) as MinionProj[];
          if (sentries.length >= this.player.maxSentries) sentries[0].dead = true;
        }
        const owner = this.player;
        this.entities.add(new MinionProj(
          px, py, cw.shoot, Math.max(1, Math.round(cwIn.damage * (ps?.dmg ?? 1))), // 基伤×词缀（乘区由 MinionProj live 结算，勿用预乘的 cw.damage）
          cw.knockback, !!cw.sentry,
          this.entities.projectiles.filter((e) => e instanceof MinionProj && !(e as MinionProj).sentry).length,
          false, // 地面型判定省略：统一飞行（地面 3 件的 walk 近似后续按 aiStyle 细分）
          () => (owner.dead ? null : owner),
        ), 'projectiles');
        // 成就：9+ 随从（Player.cs:51059 num+num2>=9 → event 6 YOU_AND_WHAT_ARMY；
        //  本作随从恒 1 槽 = 数量口径，召唤后计数）
        if (!cw.sentry
          && this.entities.projectiles.filter((e) => e instanceof MinionProj && !e.sentry).length >= 9) {
          this.achievements.handleSpecialEvent(6,
            { statLifeMax: this.player.maxHp, statManaMax: this.player.maxMana });
        }
        this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
        this.sfx.playWav('summon', 1, this.player.cx, this.player.cy); // 召唤杖 UseSound=Item44
        this.input.mouseDown = false;
        break;
      }
      case 'flail': {
        // AI_015_Flails（Projectile.cs:41051）：按住=绕体旋转,松手朝鼠标掷出,
        // 撞墙坠落→垂地悬荡→回收全状态机;同物品仅一个球在场
        const itemId = ITEM_BY_KEY[def.key];
        for (const e of this.entities.projectiles) {
          if (e instanceof FlailProj && e.projId === cw.shoot) return;
        }
        const flailP = new FlailProj(px, py, cw.damage, cw.knockback, cw.shoot,
          // ★ channel 用 mouseHeld(电平语义):mouseDown 是边沿消费量,本分支掷出后
          //   即清零,真实按住期间无新 mousedown 事件回填 → channel 恒 false,链球
          //   出生 1 帧即判松手回收消失(阳炎之怒"没起效"根因,2026-08-13)
          () => this.input.mouseHeld && !this.input.uiBlocking && this.player.inv.heldItem()?.id === itemId,
          () => { const [wx, wy] = this.camera.screenToWorld(inp.mouseX, inp.mouseY); return { x: wx, y: wy }; });
        flailP.critChance = critTotal;
        // AI_015 特例子弹生成回调(247/757/1058;entities.add 不在 GameHooks)。
        // ★sDmg 源自 FlailProj.damage=cw.damage(已含词缀乘区)——再乘 ps?.dmg
        //  = 双乘前缀(review 实锤,2026-08-13);原版 248/928/405 伤=出手时武器伤。
        // 弹型按原版 AI 形态分流(SetDefaults):
        //   248 aiStyle1 箭物理(grav 0.3,射出音 Item17 未提取 GAP);
        //   928 aiStyle14 弹跳球(34×34/grav 0.3/life 250/穿透 2,:8509);
        //   405 aiStyle70 追踪刺(ai0=-10 漂入→650 寻敌→追踪,专属 FlaironSpike)
        flailP.spawnProj = (sx, sy, svx, svy, sProj, sDmg, sKb) => {
          const dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(sDmg));
          if (sProj === 405) {
            this.entities.add(new FlaironSpike(sx, sy, svx, svy, dmg, sKb), 'projectiles');
            return;
          }
          if (sProj === 928) {
            const bub = new Arrow(sx, sy, svx, svy, dmg, sKb, sProj, null,
              { grav: 0.3, life: 250, pierce: 2, bounce: true });
            bub.w = 34; bub.h = 34; // SetDefaults width/height=34(:8509)
            this.entities.add(bub, 'projectiles');
            return;
          }
          this.entities.add(new Arrow(sx, sy, svx, svy, dmg, sKb, sProj, null,
            { grav: 0.3, life: 1200 }), 'projectiles'); // 248:aiStyle1 箭物理
        };
        flailP.armorPen = this.player.equipStats.armorPen + this.player.meleeArmorPen; // ⑥
        this.entities.add(flailP, 'projectiles');
        this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
        this.sfx.play('throw'); // UseSound=SoundID.Item1(Item.cs case 220)
        this.input.mouseDown = false; // 本击只负责掷出,旋转/掷出由 channel 回调判定
        break;
      }
      case 'yoyo': {
        // AI_099 channel：同物品已有悠悠球在场则不重复掷出（原版每把悠悠球仅一个）
        const itemId = ITEM_BY_KEY[def.key];
        for (const e of this.entities.projectiles) {
          const y = e as YoyoProj;
          if (y instanceof YoyoProj && y.projId === cw.shoot) return;
        }
        // 射程/顶速/寿命/惯性全部由 YoyoProj 实体内按 AI_099 公式 1:1 重算
        // （YOYO_RANGE/TOP/LIFE 三表 + meleeSpeed 缩放 + yoyoString 修正）——
        // 此前 Game 侧另算 stringLen/life 双轨,与实体内冲突已统一（2026-08-13）
        const yoyo = new YoyoProj(px, py, cw.damage, cw.knockback, cw.shoot, 0,
          () => this.input.mouseHeld && !this.input.uiBlocking && this.player.inv.heldItem()?.id === itemId,
          () => { const [wx, wy] = this.camera.screenToWorld(inp.mouseX, inp.mouseY); return { x: wx, y: wy }; });
        yoyo.critChance = critTotal;
        yoyo.armorPen = this.player.equipStats.armorPen + this.player.meleeArmorPen; // ⑥
        // 配重球（counterWeight：悠悠球命中时落配重投射物，原版环绕弹的直线坠落近似）
        if (this.player.equipStats.counterWeight) {
          (yoyo as unknown as { spawnWeight?: (x: number, y: number, dmg: number) => void }).spawnWeight =
            (wx, wy, wdmg) => {
              // 配重球 = 环绕实体（AI_099_1 :64472-64610 1:1——曾直线坠落 Arrow 近似）
              const cwOwn = () => (this.player.dead ? null : this.player);
              const cwAlive = () => !yoyo.dead && (yoyo as unknown as { dead: boolean }).dead === false;
              const cwE = new CounterweightProj(wx, wy, wdmg, this.player.equipStats.kbGlove ? 4 : 2,
                556 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 6), cwOwn, cwAlive, this.player.equipStats.yoyoString);
              cwE.critChance = critTotal;
              cwE.armorPen = this.player.equipStats.armorPen + this.player.meleeArmorPen;
              this.entities.add(cwE, 'projectiles');
            };
        }
        this.entities.add(yoyo, 'projectiles');
        this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
        this.sfx.play('throw');
        this.input.mouseDown = false; // 本击只负责掷出，滞留由 channel 回调判定
        break;
      }
      case 'magic': {
        // 魔法：消耗 mana（原版 mana 字段）→ 发射 item.shoot（贴图/穿透/寿命取投射物表）
        // 魔力花族：耗魔 -8%；不足时自动喝蓝重试
        const cwCost = Math.trunc(Math.round((cw.mana ?? 0) * (ps?.mcst ?? 1)) * (this.player.equipStats.manaFlower ? 0.92 : 1) * this.player.equipStats.manaCostMul); // ⑪+⑧ 词缀 mcst(I:562 round)
        if (this.player.mana < cwCost) {
          if (!(this.player.equipStats.manaFlower && this.tryAutoManaPotion() && this.player.mana >= cwCost)) {
            this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NoMana'));
            this.input.mouseDown = false;
            return;
          }
        }
        this.player.mana -= cwCost;
        const pd = projectileData(cw.shoot);
        // 彩虹枪 250（aiStyle 46）：专用弹链——隐形飞行头铺 251 彩虹段（40s 持续
        // 伤害带），通用 Arrow 无法表达铺段/恒速拱弧/静态免疫
        if (cw.shoot === 250) {
          const bolt = new RainbowBolt(px, py,
            Math.cos(ang) * cw.shootSpeed, Math.sin(ang) * cw.shootSpeed,
            cw.damage, cw.knockback);
          bolt.armorPen = this.player.equipStats.armorPen + (ps?.arpen ?? 0);
          bolt.critChance = critTotal;
          this.entities.add(bolt, 'projectiles');
          this.player.useTime = cw.useTime;
          this.player.onManaItemUsed();
          this.playUseSound(critVid, 'tink');
          this.input.mouseDown = false;
          break;
        }
        // 最后的棱镜 3541 → 633（channel 充能聚束，AI_075 :63626）：
        // 633 晶体挂手 + 6×632 光棱（PrismProj 独占链）。channel=按住（电平语义同
        // 链球 mouseHeld）；魔耗节奏 30→15→5 由 payMana 回调按拍扣
        if (cw.shoot === 633) {
          const prismOwner = this.player;
          const prismItemId = def.key;
          const prism = new PrismProj(Math.cos(ang), Math.sin(ang),
            cw.damage, cw.knockback,
            () => this.input.mouseHeld && !this.input.uiBlocking
              && prismOwner.inv.heldItem()?.id === ITEM_BY_KEY[prismItemId]
              && !prismOwner.dead,
            () => { const [wx, wy] = this.camera.screenToWorld(inp.mouseX, inp.mouseY); return { x: wx, y: wy }; },
            () => {
              const cost = Math.trunc(Math.round((cw.mana ?? 0) * (ps?.mcst ?? 1))
                * (this.player.equipStats.manaFlower ? 0.92 : 1) * this.player.equipStats.manaCostMul);
              if (this.player.mana < cost) return false;

          prism.critChance = critTotal;
          this.entities.add(prism, 'projectiles');

          this.player.onManaItemUsed();

  private toolCanBreak(type: number, power: number, toolType: string): boolean {
    const d = TILE_DEFS[type];
    if (!d) return false;
    if (toolType === 'pick') return d.pick >= 0 && power >= d.pick;
    if (toolType === 'axe') return d.axe >= 0;
    return false;
  }

  /** 原版 IsInTileInteractionRange（Player.cs:31548）+ GetTileRegion
   * （TileReachCheckSettings.cs:53）1:1：玩家盒外扩 tileRangeX=5 / tileRangeY=3
   * （+tileBoost）的【矩形】判定（Simple 档 multiplier=1/上限 20）。非圆形距离 */
  private inTileRange(tx: number, ty: number, tb = 0): boolean {
    const p = this.player;
    const x = Math.min(20, 5) + tb, y = Math.min(20, 3) + tb;
    const LX = Math.floor(p.x / TILE) - x;
    const HX = Math.ceil((p.x + p.w) / TILE) - 1 + x;
    const LY = Math.floor(p.y / TILE) - y;
    const HY = Math.ceil((p.y + p.h) / TILE) - 1 + y;
    return tx >= LX && tx <= HX && ty >= LY && ty <= HY;
  }

  /** 祭坛矿脉 OreRunner 双掷（WorldGen.cs:49095 十周年 / :49099 常规）：
   *  strength/steps 两参各独立取一次 genRand.Next(5,10)（[5,9] 闭区间）。
   *  原版不是同一掷值同传两参——单 r 同传会把"脉粗细"与"生长步数"锁死相关。 */
  static altarOreVeinRoll(rng: RNG): { strength: number; steps: number } {
    return { strength: rng.int(5, 9), steps: rng.int(5, 9) };
  }

  /** 砸祭坛 = 原版 KillTile case 26 → SmashAltar（WorldGen.cs:48949）1:1：
   *  altarCount%3 选矿档(0 钴/钯、1 秘银/山铜、2 精金/钛)，/3+1 为波次衰减；
   *  矿脉数 num3 = w/4200×310 − 85×档 ×0.85 ÷ 波次——钴档恒 ×1.05（:49008），
   *  锁定到替代矿(221/222/223)再 ×0.9（:48991 等,替代矿更稀有）；
   *  矿种按世界锁定（SavedOreTiers :48981-49030：每档首砸 roll 一次 genRand.Next(2)==0
   *  → 替代矿,此后全世界固定,随存档持久化 world.savedOreTiers——此前每砸重 roll
   *  会导致同世界混出钴+钯）；深度带按锁定矿种（:49033-49040：107/221=地表线、
   *  108/222=岩层、111/223=(岩层×2+h)/3）；每脉 OreRunner Next(5,10) 双掷恒定区间
   *  （:49095 十周年/:49099 常规——strength/steps 各独立一掷，非同一值；
   *  旧实现的 5..9+w/4200 是误读）;最后生成 1-2 只幽灵(82)。祭坛 3×2 整体移除。
   *  计数存 world.altarCount（原版 WorldGen.altarCount 静态位，WorldFile.cs:1303/2100
   *  随存档持久化——跨会话保留；海盗入侵自然 roll 的 >0 门同源 Main.cs:64938） */
  private smashAltar(tx: number, ty: number): void {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const ALTAR = TILE_BY_KEY['v_26_altars'];
    // 3×2 对象拆除（TileObjectData 语义）：frameX = style×54 + 列×18 → 列号 %3;
    // frameY/18∈{0,1} 行。锚点 = 左上格——此前扫 ±3 邻域会误拆 3 格内相邻祭坛
    const aIdx = st.idx(tx, ty);
    const ax = tx - (Math.floor(st.frameX[aIdx] / 18) % 3);
    const ay = ty - Math.floor(st.frameY[aIdx] / 18);
    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
      for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
        if (!st.inBounds(ax + dx, ay + dy)) continue;
        if (st.type[st.idx(ax + dx, ay + dy)] === ALTAR) st.setTileSilent(ax + dx, ay + dy, 0);
      }
    }
    this.chunks.markDirtyArea(ax - 1, ay - 1, ax + 3, ay + 2);
    this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 24, ty * TILE + 8, '#B080FF', 14, 2.5);
    this.sfx.play('hit', 1);
    const tier = this.world.altarCount % 3;
    const wave = Math.floor(this.world.altarCount / 3) + 1;
    // 世界锁定选种（SavedOreTiers）：每档 base/alt 二选一,首砸 roll 后固定
    const TIER_ORES: Array<{ slot: 'cobalt' | 'mythril' | 'adamantite'; base: number; alt: number }> = [
      { slot: 'cobalt', base: 107, alt: 221 },
      { slot: 'mythril', base: 108, alt: 222 },
      { slot: 'adamantite', base: 111, alt: 223 },
    ];
    const td = TIER_ORES[tier];
    if (this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot] === -1) {
      this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot] = Math.random() < 0.5 ? td.base : td.alt; // Next(2)==0 → 替代
    }
    const oreSheet = this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot];
    const ore = TILE_INTERNAL_BY_SHEET.get(oreSheet) ?? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_cobalt'];
    // 世界公告（:48991/:49008/:49021——ChatHelper misc[12/13/14 基础,+9=替代矿变体],
    // 按锁定矿种选文案）
    this.newText(Lang.misc((tier === 0 ? 12 : tier === 1 ? 13 : 14) + (oreSheet !== td.base ? 9 : 0)), 175, 75, 255);
    let veins = (st.w / 4200) * 310 - 85 * tier;
    veins *= 0.85;
    veins /= wave;
    if (tier === 0) veins *= 1.05;              // 钴档恒 ×1.05（:49008）
    if (oreSheet !== td.base) veins *= 0.9;     // 替代矿更稀有（:48991/:49016/:49029）
    const band = tier === 0 ? this.world.groundLevel : tier === 1 ? this.world.rockLevel : (this.world.rockLevel * 2 + st.h) / 3;
    const rng = new RNG((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0);
    const cap = Math.floor(veins);
    for (let k = 0; k < cap; k++) {
      const x = 100 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (st.w - 200));
      const y = Math.floor(band) + Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.max(1, st.h - 150 - Math.floor(band)));
      // 双掷（WorldGen.cs:49095 十周年 / :49099 常规）：OreRunner(i,j,strength,steps)
      // 两参各独立取一次 genRand.Next(5,10)——单 r 同传两用会把脉粗细与长度锁死相关
      const vein = Game.altarOreVeinRoll(rng);
      tileRunner(st, rng, lastGenState() ?? { worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel, lavaLine: st.h - 200, waterLine: this.world.groundLevel } as never, x, y, { strength: vein.strength, steps: vein.steps, type: ore, addTile: true });
    }
    const wraiths = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2);
    for (let k = 0; k < wraiths; k++) {
      const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(82, this.player.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 64, this.player.y - 32);
      if (e) {
        e.id = this.entities.nextId++;
        this.entities.enemies.push(e);
      }
    }
    this.world.altarCount++;
    // 成就：BEGONE_EVIL（WorldGen.cs:49111 event 6——每次砸祭坛都触发）
    this.achievements.notifyProgressionEvent(6);
  }

  /** 钻头坐骑（mount 8）每 tick 能力驱动（Mount.cs UseDrill/UseAbility/AimAbility +
   *  Player.cs ItemCheck_HandleMount :52066-52081 合并）：
   *  - 按压边沿（上 tick 双松 + 本 tick 任一按下）→ 开激光（原版伴生光标投射物 453，
   *    本仓以 mount.abilityActive 单一真源表达）；双松 → 关。
   *  - 激活期间每 tick AimAbility（二极管朝鼠标）+ UseDrill（左键挖块/右键拆墙，
   *    每束每 tick 一击，最多 2 束齐射）。
   *  挖块走 tryMine(drill)（HitTile 积累 + 镐力门槛表 1:1），拆墙走 drillPickWall。 */
  private updateDrillMountUse(): void {
    const p = this.player;
    const mnt = p.mount;
    const useItem = p.inputUseItem, useTile = p.inputUseTile;
    const pressEdge = (useItem || useTile) && !p.prevUseItem && !p.prevUseTile;
    p.prevUseItem = useItem;
    p.prevUseTile = useTile;
    if (pressEdge) mnt.setDrillActive(true);
    else if (!useItem && !useTile) mnt.setDrillActive(false);
    mnt.updateDrill();
    if (mnt.abilityActive) mnt.aimDrill(p.cx, p.cy, p.aimWX, p.aimWY);
    mnt.useDrill(p, {
      useItem,
      useTile,
      pickTile: (tx, ty) => this.tryMine(tx, ty, DRILL_PICK_POWER, 'pick', DRILL_PICK_TIME, DRILL_PICK_TIME, { drill: true }),
      pickWall: (tx, ty) => this.drillPickWall(tx, ty),
      dust: (x, y, n) => this.spawnParticles(x, y, '#9FD8FF', n, 1.4, { life: 26, grav: 0 }),
      // Tile.SmoothSlope(point, applyToNeighbors=true)（:3094）——本仓无运行期坡面平滑器，登记
    });
    this.mining = null;
  }

  /** 钻头坐骑拆墙（Player.PickWall :45328 1:1：damage 210 直接积累，≥100 即毁墙）。
   *  与锤墙路径（HitTile type-2 / 8t 冷却）不同——钻头无冷却、无 holdPound 语义 */
  private drillPickWall(tx: number, ty: number): void {
    if (!this.netCanEditTile()) return;
    const st = this.world.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return;
    const wall = st.wall[st.idx(tx, ty)];
    if (wall <= 0 || wall === 350) return;    // 350 = 不可拆墙（CanPlayerSmashWall :45305）
    const total = this.hitTiles.addDamage(tx, ty, DRILL_PICK_POWER, 2);
    this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8, '#5C4436', 2, 1.2);
    if (total >= 100) {
      this.hitTiles.clear(tx, ty, 2);
      st.setWall(tx, ty, 0);
      this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8, '#5C4436', 8, 1.8);
      this.sfx.play('chop');
    }
  }

    opts?: { drill?: boolean }) {

4. For the latch, I need to identify the Enemy by reference — vanilla stores npc index (ai[1] = index+1). In our engine, I'll store the enemy object reference plus a validity check (dead).

Now design:

**Approach**: extend Arrow with `piranha` opts. In `fixedUpdate`, when `this.piranha !== null`, run a dedicated `piranhaStep(game)` instead of the generic subStep. That keeps the generic path untouched and gives full control.

Wait, but Arrow.subStep handles: life decrement, age++, gravity, movement, tile collision, critter hits, enemy hits (with hitSet/pierce kill). The piranha needs a custom variant of most of this. Cleanest: in `fixedUpdate`, `if (this.piranha) { this.piranhaStep(game); return; }`.

The piranha state (from vanilla):
- `alpha` (255 → -50/tick, floor 0): fade-in, gates the release check & SetDummyItemTime. I'll keep a numeric `pAlpha` starting 255.
- `ai[0]`: 0 = outbound/chase, 1 = return. (Starts 0.)
- `ai[1]`: 0 = no latch (can latch on next hit), >0 = latched (enemy idx+1), -1 = released (no re-latch).
- frameCounter/frame: 4-tick cadence 4-frame loop (drawing uses age/5; I'll expose a frame override).

State machine per vanilla AI (Projectile.cs:26065-26247):

```
alpha -= 50; clamp 0
if (!active || !player.active || player.dead || dist(player, proj) > 2000) { Kill(); return; }
if (active && alpha == 0) { player.SetDummyItemTime(5); player.ChangeDir(proj.X > player.X ? 1 : -1); }
dist = |player.Center - proj.Center|
if (!player.channel && active && alpha == 0) { ai[0] = 1; ai[1] = -1; }   // release
if (ai[1] > 0 && dist > 1500) { ai[1] = 0; ai[0] = 1; }                    // leash break
if (ai[1] > 0) {
  tileCollide = false
  target = npc[ai[1]-1]
  if (target.active && target.life > 0) {
    speed = 16
    d = |target.Center - proj.Center|
    if (d < 16) {
      velocity = delta
      if (d > 16/3) { rotation/dir by velocity sign; if type 190: velocity = 0 }
    } else {
      velocity = normalize(delta) * 16; rotation/dir
    }
    if (type 190) {
      position += target.velocity
      // repulsion vs other owner's type-190 within 8px: velocity ∓ 4 per axis
    }
    ai[0] = 1
  } else {
    ai[1] = 0
    // search new target: CanBeChasedBy, dist metric = |dx|+|dy|, radius 3000, needs CanHit (line of sight)
    if (found) { velocity = normalize(to target) * 16; ai[0] = 0; ai[1] = idx+1; }
  }
} else if (ai[0] == 0) {
  if (dist > 700) { ai[0] = 1 }  // too far → return
  rotation = atan2 by velocity
} else if (ai[0] == 1) {
  tileCollide = false
  rotation (facing back)
  if (dist < 70) Kill()
  velocity = normalize(delta) * 20
  if (type 190) position += player.velocity
}
frameCounter/anim
```

Wait, careful with movement application: In vanilla Projectile.Update, position += velocity happens... where? Actually the AI sets velocity; movement integration happens after AI in Update (`position += velocity`), plus `position += npc.velocity` inside the AI for the latch-follow. In our Arrow, `this.x += this.vx` is inside subStep. For piranhaStep I'll do the same: after computing velocity, apply movement (except that when latched with velocity=0, the piranha stays glued — plus position += target.velocity each frame keeps it attached when the enemy moves).

Important detail on the latch: the piranha's hitbox follows the target via `position += npc.velocity`. The initial latch happens via contact damage (first overlap → latch). While latched, contact damage continues every 14 ticks (localNPCHitCooldown).

Another key vanilla mechanic: while latched (ai[1]>0), the piranha actively steers toward the target center at speed 16 — i.e., if the enemy moves away faster than 16/tick, the piranha chases. When within 16px, velocity = raw delta; if farther than 16/3 ≈ 5.33, it faces the enemy and zeroes velocity (bite hold). So effectively it sits on the enemy.

Now our hit/immunity model:
- Our Arrow has `hitSet` for pierce-style one-time immunity, and 502 uses meowHitCd (per-enemy cooldown map).
- For the piranha: usesLocalNPCImmunity with localNPCHitCooldown=14 → per-enemy cooldown of 14 ticks, AND `targetNPC.immune[owner] = 0` meaning the global NPC immunity vs the player is NOT set (so other player attacks can hit simultaneously). Also `npc.immune[owner] == 0` gate at Damage_PVE_Inner:11905 — `if (!(owner < 0 || targetNPC.immune[owner] == 0 || flag3)) return;` where flag3 = maxPenetrate==1 && !usesLocal && !usesIDStatic. For piranha, usesLocalNPCImmunity=true → flag3=false → the gate passes regardless of npc.immune. So the piranha hits through the enemy's global i-frames. That's important: the bite ticks ignore the enemy's global immunity.

How does our Enemy.hurt handle immunity? Let me look at Enemy.hurt to understand the cooldown/immune semantics — whether hurt() itself gates on an immunity timer and returns false. Since I need "bite ticks every 14t regardless of global immune", I need to check how hurt behaves. Let me read Enemy.hurt.

Also need to check: `hitCritters`, `hitTownNpcs` signatures in projTargets.ts, `playEnemyHitSound`.

And the "sound effect" for biting: vanilla bite has NO dedicated sound — the hit sound comes from playEnemyHitSound (NPC hit sound via StrikeNPC). The task says "periodic tick damage + sound effect" — I'll use playEnemyHitSound on each damage tick, matching vanilla (StrikeNPC plays the NPC's hit sound). Plus on tile collision: `SoundEngine.PlaySound(0, ...)` — SoundID 0. Let me check what SoundID 0 is. In Terraria.SoundID: `public const ushort Dig = 0`? Let me check the actual SoundID.cs.

Also launch sound: UseSound = SoundID.Item10 (Item.cs:14204). In our playUseSound(critVid, ...) with fallback 'bowShoot' for 'shot' kind. For item 1156 the UseSound data — check items data: does playUseSound support Item10 for 1156? There's a data-driven UseSound (582 items per memory). Let me check how playUseSound resolves and whether 1156 has UseSound=Item10 in data. If it does, the shot branch will play it automatically.

  hurt(dmg: number, kbx: number, kby: number, game: GameHooks, armorPen = 0, crit = false, pierce = 0, fromPlayer = false, penPercent = 0): boolean {
    // pierce 语义（Projectile.cs:11903-11907/13152-13155 Damage_PVE）：
    //   0   = 近战/荆棘等非弹幕 → 命中设免疫 10t（原版 immune[owner] 默认档）；
    //   1   = 单发弹（maxPenetrate==1，flag3）→ **无视免疫且命中后不设帧**
    //         ——同 tick 多发（霰弹/多随从）全部生效,不再互挡（旧全局 iframes=8
    //         致 8 发四管霰弹只吃 1 发,DPS≈1/8,2026-08-13 修）；
    //   >1  = 穿透弹 → 设 10t;同弹重复命中由弹侧 hitSet 拦（原版 per-owner 槽单人
    //         近似;弹型覆写 1-8 表 :13032 未接,登记）
    // Critter 兼容：critters 桶的调用方按 Critter.hurt(game) 单参调用；
    // 原版小动物是 Enemy 进该桶，这里把 Game 对象重映射回 game 参、伤害取固定 5
    if (typeof dmg === 'object' && dmg !== null) {
      game = dmg as unknown as GameHooks;
      dmg = 5; kbx = 0; kby = 0;
    }
    const rawDmg = dmg; // 傀儡上报用原始值（下方 def 凘认算只做一次，双端一致）
    if (pierce !== 1 && this.iframes > 0) return false; // 单发弹豁免免疫检查（flag3）
    // 旗 buff 加伤（Main.GetBannerBuffEffect → StrikeNPC 乘区,ItemID.cs:11-44 普通族:
    // 经典 ×1.5/专家 ×2;DD2 0.2 族不区分,登记）——统一入口=StrikeNPC 全通道语义
    if (this.vanillaId != null && (game as { bannerNpcActive?: (n: number) => boolean }).bannerNpcActive?.(this.vanillaId)) {
      const g = game as { world?: { flags?: Record<string, boolean> } };
      dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * (g.world?.flags?.hardMode ? 2 : 1.5)));
    }
    if (this.dontTakeDamage) return false; // 原版 NPC.dontTakeDamage（StrikeNPC 早退）
    this.justHitT = 2; // 原版 justHit 单帧旗标（AI_023 飞行武器/陆龟受击回态消费）
    // 训练假人（NPC 488，cs:83498 localAI 赋值）：immortal 永不死，
    // 受击只记摇晃强度+方向（渲染帧端消费），掉血由 dummyAI 回满
    if (this.vanillaId === 488) {
      this.dummyShake = Math.max(20, Math.min(120, dmg));
      this.dummyHitDir = Math.sign(kbx) || this.dummyHitDir;
      this.iframes = 8;
      return true;
    }
    // 猪鲨爆裂泡泡（NPC 371，StrikeNPC cs:82102-82107）：伤害清零(num=0)不扣血，
    // 直接转入爆炸态 ai[0]=1/ai[1]=4 + dontTakeDamage——"被击即爆"而非"被打死"；
    // aiStyle 70 (:35180-35194) 据此扩 100×100 并 EncourageDespawn(3) 消散
    if (this.vanillaId === 371) {
      this.ai0 = 1; this.ai1 = 4;
      this.iframes = 8; // dontTakeDamage（爆炸 4t 内不可再受击）
      return true;
    }
    // 原版 Main.CalculateDamageNPCsTake：dmg - defense*0.5，下限 1
    const def = this.def.defense ?? 0;
    // ichor（灵液药剂）：NPC.checkArmorPenetration（NPC.cs:81913-81938）——
    // armorPenetration += 15，结算 (armorPenetration/2) 整除 = +7 直伤；
    // 超防钳制（def>2×dmg 时按 dmg 上限，防穿透把伤害推到全额以上）
    // ⑥ armorPen 改减防语义（NPC.cs:81913-81938 checkArmorPenetration=min(pen,def)：
    // 有效防 = def - min(armorPen,def)，伤 = dmg - 有效防/2——此前调用方把 pen 加在
    // 伤害上约 2 倍过强；ichor 灵液仍走 +7 直伤通道（:81920 既有特例）
    const pen = this.ichorT > 0 ? 7 : 0;
    const effDef0 = def > dmg * 2 ? dmg * 2 : def;
    // 负防御(EoC P2 专家 -15/-30、诸 Boss 狂暴态)按原版直算式应为【增伤】;
    // 此前 Math.max(0,…) 把负值钳成 0 吞掉加伤(2026-08-13 对齐)。armorPen 只削
    // 非负防御(min 内侧钳 0),不与负防御交互
    // buff 侧无视防(NPC.cs:81919-81924):BrokenArmor +20 / BetsysCurse +40 armorPenetration
    const buffPen = (this.brokenArmorT > 0 ? 20 : 0) + (this.betsysCurseT > 0 ? 40 : 0);
    // armorPenetrationPercent（:12443 闪电光环 688/690=1:按比例无视防御——在定点穿甲前扣）
    const pctDef = Math.max(0, effDef0) * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, penPercent));
    const effDef = effDef0 - pctDef - Math.min(armorPen + buffPen, Math.max(0, effDef0) - pctDef);
    dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - effDef * 0.5 + pen));
    // takenDamageMultiplier（NPC.StrikeNPC cs:81997-82000）：防御结算后 >1 时 ×N

    if (this.takenDamageMultiplier > 1) dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * this.takenDamageMultiplier));
    // realLife 共享生命（StrikeNPC :82132-82137）：伤害扣主段,本段 hp/maxHp 镜像
    // ——毁灭者族打任意节都扣头 80000 总血,节不可单独打死
    if (this.realLifeHost && !this.realLifeHost.dead) {
      this.realLifeHost.hp -= dmg;
      this.hp = this.realLifeHost.hp;
      this.maxHp = this.realLifeHost.maxHp;
    } else {
      this.hp -= dmg;
    }
    // DPS 计累计(Projectile.cs:12817-12818/Player.cs:20165-20168 两调用点均

      pl?.addDPS(dmg);
      this.playerInteracted = true;  // AnyInteractions（playerInteraction 位）——雕像门 :79651
    }

    this.hpBarT = 240; // 受击后血条持续显示 4 秒
    // 击退管线（N:82144-82236 1:1）：五段软封顶 8/10/12/14/16（超出段 ×0.9/0.8/
    // 0.7/0.6/钳 16）+ 地狱火(onFire2,onFireT 代)×1.1 + 暴击×1.4 + 小丑 185×1.5。
    // ★调用侧遗留的 0.65 系数待全量清扫后此管线即完整 num3 语义（登记）

      if (imp > 10) imp = 10 + (imp - 10) * 0.8;

      if (imp > 14) imp = 14 + (imp - 14) * 0.6;
      if (imp > 16) imp = 16;

19	  24: 'onFireT',     // On Fire!(NPC.cs:92619 lifeRegen-8 = 4 HP/s)
20	  20: 'poisonT',     // Poisoned(-12 → 6 HP/s,:92590)
21	  70: 'venomT',      // Venom(-60 → 30 HP/s,:92706)
22	  39: 'cursedT',     // Cursed Inferno(onFire2,-48 → 24 HP/s,:92690)
23	  31: 'confusedT',   // Confused(TargetClosest direction 反转,:78569)
24	  69: 'ichorT',      // Ichor(防御 -15;repo 近似=armorPen 7,Enemy.ts:4132)
25	  72: 'midasT',      // Midas(掉钱 ×1.10-1.51,Enemy.ts:4286 rollCoins)
26	  44: 'frostburnT',    // Frostburn(-16 → 8 HP/s)
27	  324: 'frostburn2T',  // Frostburn2(-50 → 25 HP/s)
28	  323: 'onFire3T',     // OnFire3(-30 → 15 HP/s)
29	  153: 'shadowFlameT', // ShadowFlame(-30 → 15 HP/s)
30	  204: 'oiledT',       // Oiled:六火系 DoT 翻倍(NPC.cs:93728)
31	  36: 'brokenArmorT',  // BrokenArmor:受击无视 20 防(:81919)
32	  203: 'betsysCurseT', // BetsysCurse:受击无视 40 防(:81922)
33	  189: 'daybreakT',    // Daybreak:单层 100 HP/s(层数模型登记 GAP)
34	  30: 'bleedT',        // Bleeding:-24 = 12 HP/s(:92598)
35	  137: 'slimedT',      // Slimed:着火段叠 +4 HP/s（:92623-92624 drippingSlime）
36	  375: 'hemorrhageT',  // Hemorrhage:-200 = 100 HP/s(:92610)
37	  169: 'javelinT',     // BoneJavelin:单层 3 HP/s(层数模型 GAP)
38	  337: 'spikeT',       // TentacleSpike:单层 3 HP/s(层数模型 GAP)
39	  344: 'butcherT',     // BloodButcherer:单层 4 HP/s(层数模型 GAP)
40	};
41	
42	type StatusEnemy = { [field: string]: number | undefined };
43	
44	/** 命中结算侧调用:按投射物型号掷骰并施加 debuff(AddBuff max 合并语义)。
45	 *  duration 掷骰 Next(durMin, durMax+1) tick → 秒(/60)。
46	 *  同 mut 组 = 互斥分支(单掷骰按累积概率选一,必中其一或全落空) */
47	export function applyProjStatus(projId: number | undefined, enemy: StatusEnemy): void {
48	  if (projId === undefined || projId < 0) return;
49	  // ---- 提取器无法入表的两块手工特例（StatusNPC :10905-10915 / :10995-11002）----
50	  // type 15（魔球族,cs:10908-10914）：remixWorld → 323 否则 24；1/2 掷，300t=5s。
51	  //  remix 秘密种子未跟踪恒 false → 基档 24（OnFire 5s）
52	  if (projId === 15 && Math.random() < 0.5) {
53	    if (5 > (enemy.onFireT ?? 0)) enemy.onFireT = 5;
54	  }
55	  // type 85（彩虹魔棒弹,cs:10998-11001）：ai0==1 → 324(Frostburn2) 否则 323(OnFire3)，
56	  // 1200t=20s。实体 ai0 门未接——默认 323 档（彩虹魔棒常态档）；ai0==1 分支登记
57	  if (projId === 85) {
58	    if (20 > (enemy.onFire3T ?? 0)) enemy.onFire3T = 20;
59	  }
60	  const entries = TABLE[String(projId)];
61	  if (!entries) return;
62	  let pendingMut: { id: number; roll: number } | null = null;
63	  for (const en of entries) {
64	    let hit: boolean;
65	    if (en.mut !== undefined) {
66	      // 互斥组:同组共用一次掷骰,按累积 chance 区间判定本条是否被选中
67	      if (!pendingMut || pendingMut.id !== en.mut) pendingMut = { id: en.mut, roll: Math.random() };
68	      let lo = 0;
69	      hit = false;
70	      for (const g of entries) {
71	        if (g.mut !== en.mut) continue;
72	        const hi = lo + g.chance;
73	        if (g === en) { hit = pendingMut.roll >= lo && pendingMut.roll < hi; break; }
74	        lo = hi;
75	      }
76	    } else {
77	      hit = Math.random() < en.chance;
78	    }
79	    if (!hit) continue;
80	    const field = SUPPORTED[en.buff];
81	    if (!field) continue; // 未实装 buff,登记见上
82	    const ticks = en.durMin + Math.floor(Math.random() * (en.durMax - en.durMin + 1));
83	    const sec = ticks / 60;
84	    const cur = enemy[field] ?? 0;
85	    if (sec > cur) enemy[field] = sec;
86	  }
87	}
88	
89	/** 武器浸剂对【投射物近战族】的命中施加（StatusNPC:10559-10583 开头段:
90	 *  (melee||whip) && meleeEnchant>0 → 按药剂施加。近战挥砍侧同表在
91	 *  Game.ts 挥击结算内,此函数供 WeaponProj 四族(链球/悠悠球/长矛/回旋镖)共用)。
92	 *  时长 = Player.cs:6141-6171 Next(a,b) 秒,max 合并 */
93	/** 冰霜盔甲引擎(StatusNPC :93712-93716):(melee||ranged) 命中且套装在身 →
94	 *  AddBuff(324 Frostburn2, 60×Next(5,15) tick=5-14s,max 合并)。
95	 *  魔法/召唤弹不吃。三挂点:挥砍(Game)/WeaponProj 近战四族/远程箭 */
96	export function applyFrostBurn(frostBurn: boolean, enemy: StatusEnemy): void {
97	  if (!frostBurn) return;
98	  const sec = 5 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 11); // 60×Next(5,15)t
99	  if (sec > (enemy.frostburn2T ?? 0)) enemy.frostburn2T = sec;
100	}
101	
102	/** 岩浆石/火焰护手——近战【弹幕】命中（StatusNPC :10866-10869：melee 门，
103	 *  1/7 → OnFire3 360t=6s）。挥砍侧是另一公式（applyMagmaStoneSwing） */
104	export function applyMagmaStoneProj(magmaStone: boolean, enemy: StatusEnemy): void {
105	  if (!magmaStone || Math.random() >= 1 / 7) return;
106	  if (6 > (enemy.onFire3T ?? 0)) enemy.onFire3T = 6;
107	}
108	
109	/** 岩浆石/火焰护手——近战【挥砍】命中（Player.cs:6184-6198 三段掷）：
110	 *  1/4 → 360t=6s；否则 1/2 → 240t=4s；否则 60t=1s */
111	export function applyMagmaStoneSwing(magmaStone: boolean, enemy: StatusEnemy): void {
112	  if (!magmaStone) return;
113	  const r = Math.random();
114	  const sec = r < 0.25 ? 6 : r < 0.625 ? 4 : 1;
115	  if (sec > (enemy.onFire3T ?? 0)) enemy.onFire3T = sec;
116	}
117	
118	export function applyMeleeImbue(
119	  buffs: { has: (b: never) => boolean },
120	  enemy: StatusEnemy,
121	  partyFx?: () => void,
122	): void {
123	  const has = (bt: BuffType): boolean => buffs.has(bt as unknown as never);
124	  const max = (field: string, v: number): void => {
125	    if (v > (enemy[field] ?? 0)) enemy[field] = v;
126	  };
127	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueVenom)) max('venomT', 5 + Math.random() * 5);
128	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueCursed)) max('cursedT', 3 + Math.random() * 4);
129	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueFire)) max('onFireT', 3 + Math.random() * 4);
130	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueGold)) max('midasT', 2);
131	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueIchor)) max('ichorT', 10 + Math.random() * 10);
132	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueNano)) max('confusedT', 1 + Math.random() * 3);
133	  if (has(BuffType.ImbuePoison)) max('poisonT', 5 + Math.random() * 5);
134	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueParty)) partyFx?.();
135	}
136	

```

import sndJson from './vanilla-itemusesound.json';
import { soundTrackName } from './vanillaNpcs';
import soundTracksJson from './vanilla-soundtracks.json';

const SOUND_TRACKS = soundTracksJson as unknown as Record<string, string[]>;

/** LegacySoundStyle 别名常量（SoundID.cs:971/973/:1075）→ wav（Betsy 之 wrath 0.4 音量档
 *  与原型同源，LeafBlower=Item_34） */
const USE_SOUND_ALIASES: Record<string, string[]> = {
  DD2_BetsysWrathShot: ['dd2_betsy_fireball_shot_0', 'dd2_betsy_fireball_shot_1', 'dd2_betsy_fireball_shot_2'],
  DD2_BetsysWrathImpact: ['dd2_betsy_fireball_impact_0', 'dd2_betsy_fireball_impact_1', 'dd2_betsy_fireball_impact_2'],
  LeafBlower: ['Item_34'],
};

/** 物品 → UseSound wav 文件名；无显式赋值/无法解析返回 null。
 *  多变体声轨调用时随机取一（原版 SoundStyle.PlayVariations 语义）。 */
export function itemUseSoundWav(vid: number): string | null {
  const s = vid > 0 ? TABLE[String(vid)] : undefined;
  if (!s) return null;
  if (/^Item_\d+$/.test(s)) return s;
  if (USE_SOUND_ALIASES[s]) {
    const list = USE_SOUND_ALIASES[s];
    return list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)];
  }
  const track = soundTrackName(s);
