```ts
private piranhaStep(game: GameHooks): void {
  // life: vanilla 190 timeLeft default 3600; Arrow life decrement
  if (--this.life <= 0) { kill; return; }
  this.age++;
  const p = game.player as { cx, cy, x, y, w, h, vx?, vy?, dead, facing? } | null;
  // alpha fade (AI :26067)
  this.pAlpha = Math.max(0, this.pAlpha - 50);
  // player checks (:26071-26077)
  if (!p || p.dead) { kill; return; }
  const pd = Math.hypot(p.cx - this.cx, p.cy - this.cy);
  if (pd > 2000) { kill; return; }  // Distance > 2000 → Kill
  // channel release (:26093)
  if (this.pAlpha === 0 && !this.pChannel()) { this.pAi0 = 1; this.pAi1 = -1; }
  // latch leash (:26098)
  if (this.pAi1 > 0 && pd > 1500) { this.pAi1 = 0; this.pAi0 = 1; }
  let tileCollide = true;
  if (this.pAi1 > 0) {
    tileCollide = false;
    const t = this.pTarget; // enemy ref
    if (t && !t.dead && t.hp > 0) {
      const speed = 16;
      let dx = t.cx - this.cx, dy = t.cy - this.cy;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (d < speed) {
        this.vx = dx; this.vy = dy;
        if (d > speed / 3) {
          // face target
          if (this.vx < 0) { ... } 
          this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;   // type 190 bite hold
        }
      } else {
        this.vx = dx / d * speed; this.vy = dy / d * speed;
        // face
      }
      this.x += t.vx ?? 0; this.y += t.vy ?? 0;   // follow enemy velocity
      // repulsion vs sibling piranhas (< 8px) :26159-26178
      ...
      this.pAi0 = 1;
    } else {
      // target dead → search new (:26200-26240)
      this.pAi1 = 0;
      search: nearest enemy with line of sight within 3000 (metric |dx|+|dy|)
      if found: velocity = norm*16 toward it; ai0 = 0; ai1 = found (latch immediately)
    }
  } else if (this.pAi0 === 0) {
    if (pd > 700) this.pAi0 = 1;
  } else { // ai0 === 1 return
    tileCollide = false;
    if (pd < 70) { kill (+top-up respawn if channel held); return; }
    const s = 20;
    this.vx = (p.cx - this.cx) / pd * s; ...
    this.x += p.vx ?? 0; this.y += p.vy ?? 0;   // type 190: position += player.velocity
  }
  // animation frame (4-tick cadence, 4 frames) :26243-26252
  if (++this.pFrameCounter >= 4) { this.pFrameCounter = 0; this.pFrame = (this.pFrame + 1) % 4; }
  // movement
  this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
  // tile collision (only when tileCollide) → HandleMovement :17940: HitTiles dust + ai0=1 + PlaySound(0)
  if (tileCollide && solid at center) { ...; this.pAi0 = 1; sound; }
  // enemy contact damage + latch
  ...
}
```

// 实体基类与分桶管理器
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';

export abstract class Entity {
  id = 0;
  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
  w = 16; h = 16;
  vx = 0; vy = 0;
  dead = false;
  onGround = false;
  hitWall = false;
  hitHead = false;
  dropThrough = false;
  /** 液体位移减速因子（原版 waterMovementSpeed 族，NPC.cs:5946 默认 水/岩浆 .5、

For the target, store the enemy object reference. To be robust against the enemy being removed, also store its id and validate `!t.dead && t.hp > 0`.

**piranhaStep** flow (1:1 with vanilla order):

```ts
private piranhaStep(game: GameHooks): void {
  // local immunity tick decrement (Update :15329 DecrementLocalImmuneTimeCounters)
  for (const [k, v] of this.pHitCd) { if (v <= 1) this.pHitCd.delete(k); else this.pHitCd.set(k, v - 1); }
  if (--this.life <= 0) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }
  this.age++;
  // alpha 淡入（:26067-26071）
  this.pAlpha = Math.max(0, this.pAlpha - 50);
  const p = game.player as {...} | null;
  if (!p || p.dead) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }         // :26072
  let pdx = p.cx - this.cx, pdy = p.cy - this.cy;
  let pdist = Math.hypot(pdx, pdy);

// 原版投射物数据（tools/extract-projectiles.mjs 从 Terarria1456 Projectile.cs SetDefaults 提取）。
// 注意 1456 的 SetDefaults 是 if/else-if 链（非 switch），且长矛/悠悠球/连枷等族走
// DefaultTo*() 辅助方法（aiStyle 19/99/15）、悠悠球批量定义在 `type >= A && type <= B` 区间块。
import projJson from './vanilla-projectiles.json';

export interface ProjectileData {
  aiStyle?: number;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  penetrate?: number;
  timeLeft?: number;
  damage?: number;
  light?: number;
  /** 绘制 scale（SetDefaults，196 款非 1；绘制尺寸 = 贴图原生 × scale，非判定盒宽） */
  scale?: number;
  /** 每帧额外子步（SetDefaults，249 款非 0；总步数 = 1+extraUpdates——子弹 14=1、高速弹 207=2） */
  extraUpdates?: number;
  friendly?: boolean;
  melee?: boolean;
  ranged?: boolean;
  magic?: boolean;
  minion?: boolean;
  hostile?: boolean;
  tileCollide?: boolean;
  ignoreWater?: boolean;
}

const TABLE = projJson as unknown as Record<string, ProjectileData>;

/** 原版 projectile type → 投射物数据（无条目返回 null） */
export function projectileData(type: number): ProjectileData | null {
  return TABLE[String(type)] ?? null;
}

// ---- aiStyle 家族常量（ProjectileAIStyleIDs / 源码辅助方法实测值） ----
export const AI_ARROW = 1;        // 直飞弹（箭/子弹/大部分魔法弹）
export const AI_THROWN = 2;       // 抛物线投掷物（手里剑/飞刀，重力 0.3/tick）
export const AI_BOOMERANG = 3;    // 回旋镖（飞出后返回）
export const AI_FLAIL = 15;       // 连枷（抛掷甩链）
export const AI_GRENADE = 16;     // 弹跳爆炸物（手雷/炸弹/炸药，引信爆炸）

  /** aiStyle 39 姿态记录（:26119-26125 等：vx<0 → spriteDirection=-1 + rotation=atan2(−vy,−vx)，
   *  等效本仓 draw 的 scale(-1)+rotate(π−ang) 变换——此处存未镜像角+翻转位） */
  private pFace(vx: number, vy: number): void {
    this.pRot = Math.atan2(vy, vx);

Now the full piranhaStep:

```ts
  /** 食人鱼 190（aiStyle 39，Projectile.cs:26065-26253）状态机：
   *  ai[0]=0 直飞咬敌 / 1 返回；ai[1]=0 未咬(可咬) / >0 咬住目标 / −1 松手禁再咬 */
  private piranhaStep(game: GameHooks): void {
    // Update :15329 DecrementLocalImmuneTimeCounters——localNPCImmunity 逐帧递减
    for (const [k, v] of this.pHitCd) {
      if (v <= 1) this.pHitCd.delete(k); else this.pHitCd.set(k, v - 1);
    }
    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }
    this.age++;
    // 淡入（:26067-26071：alpha 255 起 −50/t，6t 归零）
    this.pAlpha = Math.max(0, this.pAlpha - 50);
    const p = game.player as { cx: number; cy: number; vx?: number; vy?: number; dead?: boolean; facing?: number } | null;
    if (!p || p.dead) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }          // :26072-26077
    let dxp = p.cx - this.cx, dyp = p.cy - this.cy;
    let dp = Math.hypot(dxp, dyp);
    if (dp > 2000) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }             // :26072 距主 >2000 → Kill
    // 持有期面向食人鱼（:26078-26087 alpha==0 后每帧 ChangeDir）
    if (this.pAlpha === 0 && p.facing !== undefined) p.facing = this.cx > p.cx ? 1 : -1;
    // 松手（:26093-26096）：淡入完成后断 channel → 返回 + 禁再咬（ai[1]=−1 而非 0）
    if (this.pAlpha === 0 && !this.piranha!.channel()) { this.pAi0 = 1; this.pAi1 = -1; this.pTarget = null; }
    // 咬住超距脱咬（:26098-26102：离主 >1500）
    if (this.pAi1 > 0 && dp > 1500) { this.pAi1 = 0; this.pAi0 = 1; this.pTarget = null; }
    let tileCollide = true;
    if (this.pAi1 > 0) {
      tileCollide = false;                                          // :26105
      const t = this.pTarget;
      if (t && !t.dead && t.hp > 0) {
        const speed = 16;                                           // :26109 num313
        let dx = t.cx - this.cx, dy = t.cy - this.cy;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (d < speed) {
          this.vx = dx; this.vy = dy;                               // :26116-26117
          if (d > speed / 3) {
            this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                           // :26119-26125
            this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;                               // :26126-26129 type 190 咬定
          }
        } else {
          const dd = d === 0 ? 0.0001 : d;                          // :26132-26136
          this.vx = dx / dd * speed; this.vy = dy / dd * speed;
          this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                             // :26138-26143
        }
        this.x += t.vx; this.y += t.vy;                             // :26159-26161 跟随目标速度
        // 同主多弹排斥（:26162-26178：<8px 逐轴 ∓4）
        const siblings = (game as unknown as { entities?: { projectiles?: Array<{ projId?: number; dead?: boolean; cx?: number; cy?: number; x?: number; y?: number }> } }).entities?.projectiles;
        if (siblings) {
          for (const o of siblings) {
            if (o === this || o.projId !== 190 || o.dead) continue;
            if (Math.hypot((o.cx ?? 0) - this.cx, (o.cy ?? 0) - this.cy) >= 8) continue;
            if (this.x < (o.x ?? 0)) this.vx -= 4; else this.vx += 4;
            if (this.y < (o.y ?? 0)) this.vy -= 4; else this.vy += 4;
          }
        }
        this.pAi0 = 1;                                              // :26184-26188
      } else {
        // 目标死亡 → 半径 3000 视线可达最近敌（:26197-26223，Manhattan 度量）
        this.pAi1 = 0; this.pTarget = null;                         // :26190-26196
        let best: PiranhaTarget | null = null;
        let bestScore = 3000;                                       // :26205 num319
        let bx = 0, by = 0;
        for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
          const e = ent as unknown as PiranhaTarget;
          if (e.dead || (e.hp ?? 0) <= 0 || e.dontTakeDamage) continue;   // CanBeChasedBy :91070
          const score = Math.abs(this.cx - e.cx) + Math.abs(this.cy - e.cy);
          if (score >= bestScore) continue;
          if (!canHit(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e.x, e.y, e.w, e.h)) continue;
          bestScore = score; best = e; bx = e.cx; by = e.cy;
        }
        if (best) {
          const dd = Math.hypot(bx - this.cx, by - this.cy) || 0.0001;   // :26226-26236
          this.vx = (bx - this.cx) / dd * 16; this.vy = (by - this.cy) / dd * 16;
          this.pAi0 = 0; this.pAi1 = 1; this.pTarget = best;        // :26238-26241
        }
        // 未找到 → ai[0] 已被 :26188 置 1 → 返回（即便仍按住）
      }
    } else if (this.pAi0 === 0) {
      if (dp > 700) this.pAi0 = 1;                                  // :26247-26251
      this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                                 // :26252-26261
    } else {
      tileCollide = false;                                          // :26266
      this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                                 // :26267-26287
      if (dp < 70) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }              // :26276-26279 回收
      const dd = dp === 0 ? 0.0001 : dp;                            // :26273 num324=20
      this.vx = dxp / dd * 20; this.vy = dyp / dd * 20;
      this.x += p.vx ?? 0; this.y += p.vy ?? 0;                     // :26288-26290 type 190
    }
    // 帧动画（:26243-26252）：frameCounter ≥4 推帧，4 帧循环
    ...

pTopUp:
```ts
  /** 补弹（Player.cs:47768-47786 ItemCheck_Shoot 1156 专支：生成 3−在场数 条，
   *  速度分量各 ±Next(-40,41)×0.05 扰动；出生点=玩家枪口） */
  private pTopUp(game: GameHooks): void {
    const p = game.player as { cx: number; cy: number } | null;
    const add = (game as unknown as { entities?: { projectiles?: unknown[]; add?: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;
    if (!p || !add?.add) return;
    const alive = (add.projectiles ?? []).filter(
      (o) => (o as { projId?: number }).projId === 190 && !(o as { dead?: boolean }).dead).length;
    const aim = this.piranha!.aim();
    const a = Math.atan2(aim.y - p.cy, aim.x - p.cx);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3 - alive; i++) {
      const np = new Arrow(p.cx + Math.cos(a) * 14, p.cy - 4 + Math.sin(a) * 14,
        Math.cos(a) * 14 + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) - 40) * 0.05,
        Math.sin(a) * 14 + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) - 40) * 0.05,
        this.damage, this.knockback, 190, null,
        { grav: 0, life: 3600, piranha: this.piranha! });
      np.critChance = this.critChance; np.critBonus = this.critBonus;
      np.armorPen = this.armorPen; np.frostEligible = this.frostEligible;

[]).filter(\n      (o) => (o as { projId?: number }).projId === PIRANHA_PROJ && !(o as { dead?: boolean }).dead).length;\n    const aim = this.piranha!.aim();\n    const a = Math.atan2(aim.y - p.cy, aim.x - p.cx);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 3 - alive; i++) {\n      const np = new Arrow(p.cx + Math.cos(a) * 14, p.cy - 4 + Math.sin(a) * 14,\n        Math.cos(a) * this.pShootSpeed + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) - 40) * 0.05,\n        Math.sin(a) * this.pShootSpeed + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) - 40) * 0.05,\n        this.damage, this.knockback, PIRANHA_PROJ, null,\n        { grav: 0, life: PIRANHA_LIFE, piranha: this.piranha! });\n      np.critChance = this.critChance;\n      np.critBonus = this.critBonus;\n      np.armorPen = this.armorPen;\n      np.frostEligible = this.frostEligible;\n      np.pShootSpeed = this.pShootSpeed;\n      ents.add(np, 'projectiles');\n    }\n  }\n\n  private piranhaStep(game: GameHooks): void {\n    // Update :15329 DecrementLocalImmuneTimeCounters——localNPCImmunity 逐帧递减\n    for (const [k, v] of this.pHitCd) {\n      if (v <= 1) this.pHitCd.delete(k); else this.pHitCd.set(k, v - 1);\n    }\n    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }\n    this.age++;\n    // 淡入（:26067-26071）\n    this.pAlpha = Math.max(0, this.pAlpha - 50);\n    const p = game.player as { cx: number; cy: number; vx?: number; vy?: number; dead?: boolean; facing?: number } | null;\n    // :26072-26077 玩家失活/死亡/离主 >2000 → Kill\n    if (!p || p.dead) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }\n    const dxp = p.cx - this.cx, dyp = p.cy - this.cy;\n    const dp = Math.hypot(dxp, dyp);\n    if (dp > 2000) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }\n    // :26078-26087 淡入完成后每帧面向食人鱼（SetDummyItemTime 持物锁未接，登记 GAP）\n    if (this.pAlpha === 0 && p.facing !== undefined) p.facing = this.cx > p.cx ? 1 : -1;\n    // :26093-26096 松手（淡入完成后才生效）：返回 + ai[1]=−1 禁再咬\n    if (this.pAlpha === 0 && !this.piranha!.channel()) {\n      this.pAi0 = 1; this.pAi1 = -1; this.pTarget = null;\n    }\n    // :26098-26102 咬住超距（离主 >1500）脱咬转返回（ai[1]=0 仍可再咬）\n    if (this.pAi1 > 0 && dp > 1500) { this.pAi1 = 0; this.pAi0 = 1; this.pTarget = null; }\n    let tileCollide = true;\n    if (this.pAi1 > 0) {\n      tileCollide = false; this.vy = dy;                        // :26116-26117 残差直抵\n          if (d > speed / 3) {\n            this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                    // :26119-26125 朝目标\n            this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;                        // :26126-26129 type 190 咬定\n          }\n        } else {\n          const dd = d === 0 ? 0.0001 : d;                   // :26132-26136\n          this.vx = dx / dd * speed; this.vy = dy / dd * speed;\n          this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy); dead?: boolean; x?: number; y?: number; cx?: number; cy?: number }> };\n        }).entities?.projectiles;\n        if (sibs) {\n          for (const o of sibs) {\n            if (o === this || o.projId !== PIRANHA_PROJ || o.dead) continue;\n            if (Math.hypot((o.cx ?? 0) - this.cx, (o.cy ?? 0) - this.cy) >= 8) continue;\n            if (this.x < (o.x ?? 0)) this.vx -= 4; this.pTarget = null;\n        // :26197-26223 半径 3000 内视线可达最近敌（CanBeChasedBy :91070 近似 =\n        //  存活/未无敌/可受伤；Manhattan 度量 num322）\n        let best: PiranhaTarget | null = null;\n        let bestScore = 3000;                                // :26205 num319\n        let bx = 0, by = 0;\n        for (const ent of game.enemies()) {\n          const e = ent as unknown as PiranhaTarget;\n          if (e.dead || e.hp <= 0 || e.dontTakeDamage) continue;\n          const score = Math.abs(this.cx - e.cx) + Math.abs(this.cy - e.cy);\n          if (score >= bestScore) continue;\n          if (!canHit(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e.x, e.y, e.w, e.h)) continue;\n          bestScore = score; best = e; bx = e.cx; by = e.cy;\n        }\n        if (best) {\n          // :26223-26241 转咬新目标（立即入咬住态，速度 16 直指）\n          const dd = Math.hypot(bx - this.cx, by - this.cy) || 0.0001;\n          this.vx = (bx - this.cx) / dd * 16;\n          this.vy = (by - this.cy) / dd * 16;\n          this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);\n          this.pAi0 = 0; this.pAi1 = 1; this.pTarget = best;\n        }\n        // 未找到新目标 → pAi0 已被 :26188 段置 1 → 返回（即便仍按住）\n      }\n    } else if (this.pAi0 === 0) {\n      // :26245-26261 直飞：无追踪（首咬靠接触），离主 >700 → 返回\n      if (dp > 700) this.pAi0 = 1;                           // :26247-26251\n      this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                          // :26252-26261\n    } else {\n      // :26266-26299 返回：穿墙、速度 20 直指玩家、<70px 回收\n      tileCollide = false;\n      this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy); this.y += p.vy ?? 0;              // :26296-26299 type 190\n    }\n    // :26301-26310 帧推进（4t/帧 4 帧循环；AI 侧真源，draw 消费 pFrame）\n    if (++this.pFrameCounter >= 4) {\n      this.pFrameCounter = 0;\n      this.pFrame = (this.pFrame + 1) % 4;\n    }\n    // 位移积分（Update 运动段在 AI 后）\n    this.x += this.vx;\n    this.y += this.vy;\n    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);\n    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);\n    if (!game.world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }\n    // 撞块（HandleMovement :17940-17964 aiStyle 39 档：HitTiles 尘 + dig 声 +\n    //  ai[0]=1 返回，弹体不消亡；咬住/返回期 tileCollide=false 整段跳过）\n    const tileType = game.world.store.get(tx, ty);\n    if (tileCollide && tileType !== 0) {\n      game.cutTile(tx, ty);\n      if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) {\n        this.x -= this.vx; this.y -= this.vy;                // 碰撞回退（停在墙前）\n        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;\n        this.pAi0 = 1;                                       // :17958 返回\n        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#B0A080', 4, 1.5, { life: 18 });\n        game.playSfx('dig', 1, this.cx, this.cy);            // :17962 SoundID.Dig(0)\n      }\n    }\n    // 小动物接触（Damage_PVE 对 NPC 通用：小动物 friendly=false 亦受击；\n    //  penetrate=−1 弹体不消耗；非 CanBeChasedBy 目标不会成为咬住对象）\n    hitCritters(this, game);\n    // 命中结算（Damage_PVE :11850 迭代 + Damage_PVE_Inner :11888）\n    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {\n      const e = ent as unknown as PiranhaTarget;\n      if (e.dead) continue;\n      if (!(this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x\n        && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y)) continue;\n      if (this.pHitCd.has(e.id)) continue;                   // :11869 localNPCImmunity 门\n      if ((e.iframes ?? 0) > 0) continue;                    // :11905 npc.immune[owner] 门\n      // StrikeNPC :12811（暴击链同通用弹：基 4% + spawn 注入系暴击）\n      const crit = Math.random() < (this.critChance || (4 + this.critBonus) / 100);\n      const dmg = crit ? this.damage * 2 : this.damage;\n      // StatusNPC :10622-10625：命中必挂 Hemorrhage(375) 240-360t（表驱动）\n      applyProjStatus(PIRANHA_PROJ, e as unknown as Record<string, number>);\n      if (this.frostEligible) {\n        applyFrostBurn((game.player as unknown as { equipStats?: { frostBurn?: boolean } }).equipStats?.frostBurn,\n          e as unknown as Record<string, number>);\n      }\n      playEnemyHitSound(game, e);\n      // pierce=1 档：命中不设敌方免疫帧（:13154-13158 immune[owner]=0 语义），\n      // fromPlayer=true 走 DPS/掉落门链\n      e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(e.cx - this.cx) * this.knockback, 0, game, this.armorPen, crit, 1, true);\n      game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.cy, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');\n      this.pHitCd.set(e.id, PIRANHA_HIT_CD);                 // :13157 localNPCHitCooldown=14\n      if (this.pAi1 === 0) { this.pAi1 = 1; this.pTarget = e; }   // :12447-12450 首咬 latch\n    }\n  }\n}"

```
export function hitCritters(self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, game: GameHooks): boolean {
  const list = (game as unknown as { critters?: () => unknown[] }).critters?.() ?? [];
  for (const ent of list) {
    const c = ent as CritterLike;
    if (c.dead) continue;
    if (projOverlap(self.x, self.y, self.w, self.h, c)) {
      c.hurt(game);
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** 弹幕命中怪物:播受击音效(原版 NPC.StrikeNPC 语义——任意伤害来源都响,
 *  近战挥砍早已如此;弹幕此前漏了) */
export function playEnemyHitSound(game: GameHooks, e: { def?: { hitSound?: string[] } }): void {
  const files = e.def?.hitSound;
  if (files && files.length) game.playSfxFiles(files, 0.9);
}

/** 敌对弹幕命中玩家(原版 Projectile.Damage_EVP,Projectile.cs:13706-13830):
 *  - 门禁 hostile + damage>0(:13708)——调用方用 hostile 标志控制,本函数只做判定;
 *  - 玩家 dead / 无敌即跳过(:13732 player.immune)——Player.damage 自带 iframes,
 *    与原版 immune 同义去重,无需调用方另记冷却;
 *  - 相交判定 = AABB 重叠(Colliding :13749,Rect.Intersects 同构);
 *  - 伤害结算走 GameHooks.damagePlayer(= Player.Hurt 入口,含防御减算/击退/
 *    受击音/伤害数字/饰品触发)。
 *  原版敌弹命中【不消耗穿透】(:13811-13829 仅 435/436/437/681/682 特判递减),
 *  弹体照常飞行——调用方不得据此销毁弹体。
 *  返回是否【实际造成伤害】(iframes/闪避吞掉时 false,对应原版 Hurt 返回 0;
 *  StatusPlayer 的 debuff 授予以此为准,:13798-13800)。 */

## 方案与理由
**扩展 Arrow**（`opts.piranha` + `fixedUpdate` 分流入 `piranhaStep`），不新建实体：Arrow 已备齐 190 全部基建（projectileData 判定盒 22×22、`PROJ_ROT_RIGHT` 朝右贴图 + `projFrameImg` 四帧行、`killNow→onProjectileDeath`、hurt 链先例）；Game.ts 侵入 = 一处 if + 一个 import；补弹走 PrismProj 已确立的 `entities.add` cast 先例留在实体侧。

## 状态机行号锚（Terarria1456）
- **SetDefaults** `Projectile.cs:2509-2520`（22×22/aiStyle39/penetrate −1/alpha 255/localNPCHitCooldown=14），timeLeft 通用默认 3600（:526）；物品 1156 `Item.cs:14194-14207`（channel/damage 40/knockBack 1/shootSpeed 14/UseSound Item_10）
- **AI 39** `Projectile.cs:26065-26315`：淡入 −50/t（26067）→ 玩家死/离主>2000 Kill（26072）→ 松手 ai[0]=1+ai[1]=−1 且 **alpha==0 门**（26093）→ 咬住超距>1500 脱咬（26098）→ 咬住段 16 速逼近（26109）/<16 残差直抵 + >16/3 咬定清速（26115-26129）/跟随目标速度（26159）/同主多弹<8px 逐轴∓4（26162-26178）/ai[0] 恒置 1（26184）→ 目标死亡后 3000 内视线可达最近敌（Manhattan，26205/26212）转咬速 16（26223-26241）→ 直飞段**无追踪**（26245，首咬靠接触）离主>700 返回（26247）→ 返回段穿墙/速 20/<70 回收（26266-26299）→ 帧推进 4t/帧（26301）
- **命中链**：latch `:12447-12456`（ai[1]==0 才咬，−1 即禁再咬）、localNPCImmunity 门 `:11869`、全局 immune 门 `:11905`、冷却写入 `:13157`（immune[owner]=0 + 14t）、StatusNPC 挂 Hemorrhage(375) `:10622-10625`、冷却递减 `:15934`（Update:15329 先于 AI）
- **撞块** `HandleMovement:17940-17964`：HitTiles 尘 + ai[0]=1 返回 + SoundID.Dig(0)（SoundID.cs:21），弹体不消亡
- **发射/补弹** `Player.cs:42856-42868`（channel 期 num∈(0,3) 补射）+ `:47768-47786`（生成 3−num 条）

## 与 PROJ_ROT_RIGHT 的协同
190 在朝右贴图集合内（rotation=atan2(vy,vx)+左行镜像）。AI 侧把原版 (spriteDirection, rotation) 对折算为**未镜像角 pRot + 翻转位 pFlip**，三处 rotation 赋值统一走 `pFace()`，draw 用 pFlip 替代 `vx<0` 判镜像。关键：咬住时 velocity 清零（:26126-26129），draw 现算 atan2(0,0)=0 会把鱼掰回正右——原版 rotation 取的是清速前目标向，必须 AI 侧记录。帧号也改由 AI 推进（pFrame 4t/帧）替代通用 age/5 档，淡入 alpha 接进 globalAlpha。

## 遗留偏差（均登记在代码注释）
1. `SetDummyItemTime(5)` 持物锁未接（:26079）——按住期玩家可自由行动/切枪（切枪断 channel → 返回，主语义保留）；ChangeDir 面向已接
2. `AI_AdjustPlayerItemRotationToFaceProjectile`（:26311-261314）未接——持枪不实时转向食人鱼，纯视觉
3. CanBeChasedBy（:91070）近似：以 存活/未 dontTakeDamage 代替完整 chaseable/lifeMax>5/!immortal（训练假人等 immortal 态可能被选为转咬目标）
4. 撞块用"取消本帧位移"近似 HandleMovement 逐轴解析（转向返回语义一致）
5. 小动物接触一击致死但不消耗弹体、不成为咬住对象（原版会 latch 一帧濒死小动物，跳过）
```
